TL;DR: 本文讨论了由于磁场不均匀性导致的同步辐射光谱畸变。研究表明，对于单粒子辐射，仅当观测时间显著超过束流图样偏离时间且粒子为亚相对论性时，均匀磁场与非均匀磁场的积分频谱才存在明显差异；对于更短的观测时间，不均匀性只导致小尺度畸变。对于天体物理应用中的粒子系综，积分频谱差异可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁场不均匀性对同步辐射光谱的影响，分析在何种条件下这种不均匀性会导致观测光谱与均匀磁场假设下的显著差异，以评估天体物理观测中均匀磁场近似的有效性。

Method: 通过理论分析分别讨论单粒子辐射和粒子系综两种情况，比较不同观测时间尺度下非均匀磁场与均匀磁场中同步辐射积分频谱的差异。

Result: 单粒子辐射中，仅当观测时间远大于束流图样偏离时间且粒子为亚相对论性时，非均匀磁场才会导致明显的光谱差异；短观测时间下仅出现小尺度（频率量级不超过回旋频率）畸变。粒子系综情况下，非均匀磁场与均匀磁场的积分频谱差异可忽略。

Conclusion: 在天体物理应用中，磁场不均匀性对同步辐射的积分光谱影响极小，均匀磁场近似在多数情况下是合理的。

Abstract: The distortion of the spectrum of synchrotron radiation received by a distant observer due to magnetic field inhomogeneity is discussed. It is shown that for single-particle radiation, a noticeable difference in the integrated spectrum from the case of a uniform magnetic field occurs only for observation times significantly exceeding the observer's departure from the beam pattern, and only for subrelativistic particles, when the radiation arrives from regions with significantly different magnetic fields during the observation time. For shorter observation times, field inhomogeneity leads only to small-scale distortions (smaller or comparable in frequency to the gyrofrequency) and does not manifest itself in the averaged spectrum. For the case of a particle ensemble, which is of interest for astrophysical applications, the difference in the integrated spectrum becomes negligible.

</details>

Method: 测试了角功率谱和自相关函数两种方法对不同尺度各向异性的灵敏度，并应用于KASCADE实验10%公开数据（包含全粒子事件集和卷积神经网络分类的各质量组事件集）。

Result: 自相关函数在大多数测试模型中对物理各向异性最敏感；角功率谱在各向异性足够强时可提供额外信息。在KASCADE数据中，铁核组（能量≥20 PeV）在约10°角尺度上检测到>2.5 σ的各向异性信号。

Conclusion: 自相关函数是探测中小尺度宇宙线各向异性的有效工具，角功率谱可作为辅助方法；两种方法联合使用可在实际数据中发现新的各向异性特征。

Abstract: We study the problem of measuring the anisotropy of high-energy cosmic rays across all angular scales. The limited field of view and non-uniform exposure of ground-based cosmic-ray experiments reduce their sensitivity to real anisotropy of cosmic-ray arrival directions. A widely used signature of anisotropy -- a dipole of the flux expansion over the right ascension -- provides very limited understanding of the underlying physics of cosmic-ray origin and propagation. In this study we test two other methods: the angular power spectrum and the autocorrelation function for sensitivity to possible medium- and small-scale anisotropies of cosmic-ray flux. We find that the autocorrelation function is the most sensitive estimator for an underlying physical anisotropy in most of the models tested, while the angular power spectrum can provide additional knowledge about cosmic-ray flux properties, when the anisotropy is strong enough. As a test of our findings, we apply these methods to $10\%$ sample of the KASCADE experiment public data. Namely, we consider all-particle set of events and sets of individual mass groups classified by a convolutional neural network. We find an indication of anisotropy at $> 2.5 σ$ level at $\sim 10^\circ$ angular scale for the iron nuclei mass group at $E \gtrsim 20$ PeV with both the angular power spectrum and the autocorrelation methods.

Conclusion: 下一代VLBI阵列具备同时分辨M 87超大质量黑洞吸积流、喷流发射及加速区的独特能力，能够通过多频观测辨别不同的电子加热模型和粒子分布模型。

Abstract: Next generation Very Long Baseline Interferometers (VLBI) will provide dense sampling of the Fourier space together with high signal to noise ratios allowing to reliably observe and image faint jet structure in M 87 at mm-wavelength. The proposed next generation Event Horizon Telescope (ngEHT) and next generation Very Large Array (ngVLA) offers the unique capability to simultaneously resolve and image the accretion flow around the supermassive black hole in M 87 together with the jet launching and acceleration zone. In order to explore these capabilities and to provide theoretical expectations we perform general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations of accretion on to black holes and jet launching. M 87 has been the target for multiple observations across the entire electromagnetic spectrum. Among these VLBI observations provide unique capability to resolve the jet structure down to several gravitational radii. In this work we provide possible observable signatures which will allow us to distinguish between different electron heating models and particle distributions. We use general relativistic magnetohydrodynamics and simulate the accretion of the magnetised plasma onto Kerr-black holes in 3D. The multi-frequency radiative signatures of these simulations are computed taking different electron heating and distribution functions into account. The results of our simulations show that with a dynamical range of $1\times 10^4$ and a frequency range from 86 GHz to 345 GHz observations with future VLBI arrays have the potential to tell turbulent and magnetic reconnection electron heating and the electron distribution function apart.

</details>


### [95] [Discovering Millisecond Pulsars in Globular Clusters with the GMRT (GCGPS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11461)
*Jyotirmoy Das,Jayanta Roy,Paulo C. C. Freire,Scott M Ransom,Bhaswati Bhattacharyya,Karel Adámek,Wes Armour,Sanjay Kudale,Mekhala V. Muley*

Abstract: The Globular Clusters GMRT Pulsar Search (GCGPS) project, launched in mid-May 2023, has emerged as one of the most successful pulsar surveys conducted with the upgraded Giant Metrewave Radio Telescope (uGMRT), leading to the discovery of several new millisecond pulsars (MSPs). The recently concluded Phase I of the survey resulted in the discovery of seven MSPs across four globular clusters (GCs), all of which previously had no known pulsars. These discoveries have enabled the precise determination of the dispersion measures (DMs) for the respective clusters for the first time. Consistent timing follow-up revealed that out of the seven MSPs, three are confirmed binaries, while two are isolated. This paper summarises the design and the implementation of Phase I of the GCGPS project, presents the key scientific results obtained so far, and outlines the strategy and progress of the recently initiated Phase II observations.

</details>


### [96] [Superdiffusion of cosmic rays in the vicinity of their accelerators and the resulting $γ$-ray emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11485)
*Zhaodong Shi,Guangwei Wang,Rui-zhi Yang*

TL;DR: 本文研究宇宙线在加速器附近超扩散输运（超越正常扩散）的分布特征，发现超扩散参数α显著影响宇宙线空间分布：脉冲注入时呈常数径向分布（α<2为幂律尾，α=2为高斯尾），稳态注入时质子呈r^{α-3}分布而电子因能量损失偏离该分布；通过分析100 pc内γ射线形态，可利用当前及下一代成像大气切伦科夫望远镜区分超扩散与正常扩散。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宇宙线输运模型通常假设正常扩散，但实际星际介质中的湍流和磁场结构可能导致超扩散行为。本文旨在系统研究超扩散对宇宙线在加速器附近分布的影响，并探索通过γ射线观测区分超扩散与正常扩散的可能性。

Result: 超扩散显著改变宇宙线分布：脉冲注入下径向分布呈常数平台加尾部（α<2幂律尾，α=2高斯尾）；稳态注入下质子分布正比于r^{α-3}，电子因能量损失偏离此关系。γ射线形态分析表明，通过成像大气切伦科夫望远镜可区分超扩散与正常扩散。

Conclusion: 超扩散输运是理解加速器附近宇宙线分布的关键因素，γ射线观测为检验输运机制提供了可行途径，对当前和下一代成像大气切伦科夫望远镜具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We study the distribution of cosmic rays (CRs) in the vicinity of their accelerators, assuming that the transport of CRs in the interstellar medium surrounding the accelerators is described by the superdiffusion, beyond the normal diffusion. We find that the superdiffusivity, which is characterized by the superdiffusion parameter $α$, impacts significantly the distribution of CRs. For impulsive injection, the CR distribution behaves a constant radial profile, except with a power-law tail for $α< 2$ or a Gaussian tail for $α=2$ at large distance, $r$, from the accelerators. For stationary injection, the radial profile of CR protons tends to being proportional to $r^{α- 3}$, while that of CR electrons can deviate from the $r^{α- 3}$ profile, due to their severe energy losses. We also compute the $γ$-ray emission, produced by the interactions of CRs with ambient gas and radiation fields, within 100 pc regions around the accelerators. We find that by investigating the $γ$-ray morphology, we can distinguish the superdiffusion from the normal diffusion with present and next-generation imaging air Cherenkov telescopes.

</details>


### [97] [Two Earliest Optical-UV Tidal Disruption Events Hidden in the SDSS DR7 Catalog Unveiled by the Transformer-Based Spectrum Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11539)
*Ranfang Zheng,Zheyu Lin,Xu Kong,Ran Song,Dezheng Meng,Lulu Fan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主成分分析增强的Transformer分类器，用于从大规模光学光谱星表中识别潮汐瓦解事件（TDE），在SDSS DR7中发现了两例新TDE和一例疑似TDE，其中SDSS J124225.39+642919.0是目前已知最早的光学-紫外TDE（MJD < 52316，2002年2月11日之前），展示了机器学习分类器在挖掘大体积星表数据中隐藏TDE的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学-紫外TDE的识别高度依赖光谱特征，而理论和观测估计的TDE发生率仅为10^{-5}-10^{-4}星系^{-1}年^{-1}，意味着大规模光谱星表（>10^5条星系光谱）中可能混杂着未被发现的TDE光谱。因此，需要构建有效的选择方法从海量数据中挖掘这些"埋藏的宝藏"。

Method: 采用主成分分析（PCA）增强的Transformer模型作为TDE光谱分类器，在评估数据集上实现了0.88的精确率和0.99的召回率。将该分类器应用于广泛使用的SDSS DR7星表进行搜索。

Result: 在SDSS DR7中发现了两例新TDE（SDSS J124225.39+642919.0和SDSS J152459.70+045423.1）及一例报告疑似TDE。SDSS J124225.39+642919.0的紫外瞬变在GALEX星表中得到确认，发生时间早于光谱观测时间（MJD < 52316，2002年2月11日），成为目前已知最早的光学-紫外TDE。SDSS J152459.70+045423.1的光谱符合TDE-H+He光谱的所有特征，其光学爆发记录始于54269 < MJD < 54476（2007年6月18日-2008年1月11日），属于最早报告的光学TDE之一。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的分类器在大体积星表中挖掘光学-紫外TDE方面具有强大能力，为TDE发现提供了一种新方法。

Abstract: Optical spectroscopic features are decisive in the current identification of optical-UV tidal disruption events (TDEs). Regarding that the TDE estimated occurrence rate is $10^{-5}-10^{-4}$ galaxy$^{-1}$ yr$^{-1}$ by both theoretical and observational methods, large optical spectroscopic catalogs with >10$^5$ galaxy spectra can include some serendipitous spectra with TDE spectroscopic features, which can be found after building a useful selection method. We hereby introduce a principal component analysis enhanced Transformer TDE spectrum classifier which achieves a precision of 0.88 and a recall of 0.99 on our evaluation dataset, and report its inspiring discoveries in the widely-used SDSS DR7 catalog: two newly discovered TDEs and one reported likely TDE. For SDSS J124225.39+642919.0, we confirm the presence of a UV transient in GALEX catalog when the spectrum was taken, and its occurrence time should be earlier than the spectrum observation time, MJD < 52316 (February 11, 2002), making it the earliest optical-UV TDE discovered by now. For SDSS J152459.70+045423.1, its spectrum matches all features of the TDE-H+He spectrum, and was taken during an optical outburst recorded by the Catalina Real-time Transient Survey. The start of this outburst lies in 54269 < MJD < 54476 (June 18, 2007 - January 11, 2008), making it one of the earliest among the reported optical TDEs. The discovery of two new TDEs highlights the power of machine-learning based classifiers in digging out buried treasures in large-volume catalogs, and marks a new method for discovering optical-UV TDEs.

</details>


### [98] [AT2019ijn: a fast-rising, slow-decaying blue optical transient with exceptionally bright radio emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11545)
*Hucheng Ding,Xinwen Shu,Luming Sun,Liangduan Liu,Lei Yang,Yunwei Yu,Xueguang Zhang,Ying Gu,Fangkun Peng,Fabao Zhang,Zhumao Zhang,Ningyu Tang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: AT2019ijn是一个发生在矮星系核区、红移z=0.273的特殊光学暂现源。它在5天内快速增亮至Mg=-21.1的峰值，随后缓慢衰减超过一个月，期间保持约1.5-1.6×10^4 K的高黑体温度。其射电辐射尤为突出，在光学发现后640天达到峰值，光度达2×10^31 erg/s/Hz，比已知的快速蓝色光学暂现源和超新星在相似时刻的亮度高出至少两个数量级，与喷流式潮汐瓦解事件相当。这种明亮且持久的射电辐射可用视角约40度的离轴相对论性喷流解释。作者倾向于认为这是一个中等质量黑洞（约10^5 Msun）引发的喷流式潮汐瓦解事件，但喷流磁星模型不能完全排除。AT2019ijn代表了一类新的相对论性光学暂现源，凸显了射电巡天在发现离轴喷流事件中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现并表征AT2019ijn这一特殊光学暂现源，其射电辐射特性显著不同于已知的快速蓝色光学暂现源和超新星，而与喷流式潮汐瓦解事件相当，可能代表了一类新的相对论性暂现源。

Method: 通过光学和射电多波段观测对AT2019ijn进行光变曲线、能谱及物理建模分析，利用黑体拟合估计温度，结合相对论性喷流模型（视角约40度）解释其射电辐射时延和光度特征。

Result: AT2019ijn在5天内达到Mg=-21.1的峰值光度，衰减期超过一个月，保持约1.5-1.6×10^4 K的高温；射电辐射在640天后达到峰值（2×10^31 erg/s/Hz），比已知同类暂现源亮至少两个数量级；最佳解释为中等质量黑洞（~10^5 Msun）引发的离轴喷流式潮汐瓦解事件。

Conclusion: AT2019ijn代表了一类全新的相对论性光学暂现源，强调射电巡天对于发现离轴喷流事件的关键作用，喷流式潮汐瓦解事件是最可能的起源，但喷流磁星模型尚未被完全排除。

Abstract: We report the discovery of a peculiar optical transient, AT2019ijn, occurred in the nuclear region of a dwarf galaxy at z=0.273. It rises rapidly to peak at a luminosity of Mg=-21.1 in 5 days, followed by a slow decline over more than a month, during which the optical emission has a persistently high blackbody temperature of T_BB~1.5-1.6x10^4 K. The radio emission is exceptional which peaks at 640 days after optical discovery with a high luminosity of 2x10^31 erg/s/Hz. The peak radio luminosity is at least two orders of magnitude brighter than known radio-bright fast blue optical transients and supernova explosions at similar epochs, but comparable to jetted tidal disruption events. The luminous and long-lasting radio emission with a late-time peak can be explained by an off-axis relativistic jet with a viewing angle of ~40 deg. We discuss possible origins for AT2019ijn and favor a jetted tidal disruption event involving an intermediate-mass black hole of ~10^5 Msun, although a jetted magnetar model cannot be fully ruled out. AT2019ijn represents a new class of relativistic optical transients that highlights the importance of radio surveys for discovering off-axis jetted events.

</details>


### [99] [Radio and X-ray flux rebrightening six years after outburst in a partially-obscured extreme changing-look AGN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11546)
*Tianyao Zhou,Xinwen Shu,Lei Yang,Tao Wu,Luming Sun,Yibo Wang,Guobin Mou,Ning Jiang,Wenjie Zhang,Hucheng Ding,Fabao Zhang,Yujun Yao,Liming Dou,Yogesh Chandola,Ningyu Tang,Jianguo Wang,Tinggui Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对SDSS J1548+2208这一部分遮蔽的核瞬变事件进行了约2500天的多波段观测，发现其中红外、X射线仍处高态，射电SED呈现双峰结构且存在晚期再增亮现象，支持其为极端变脸AGN而非TDE，并可能驱动射电外流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDSS J1548+2208是一个独特的部分遮蔽核瞬变事件，表现为多波段爆发，但其物理起源（TDE还是变脸AGN）尚不明确，需要长期多波段监测以揭示其本质。

Method: 对SDSS J1548+2208进行了约2500天的多波段测光和光谱后随观测，分析其中红外、X射线、射电波段的光变、颜色演化、光谱特征及射电SED形态。

Result: 中红外和X射线仍处高态，硬X射线谱；中红外颜色在上升期缓慢变蓝，区别于TDE；射电SED呈双峰结构且变异性不同；存在晚期（>6年）射电再增亮及可能的X射线新耀发证据。

Conclusion: SDSS J1548+2208的爆发起源于极端变脸AGN，而非正常TDE；其特殊的射电光变和SED演化可用新生外流撞击富含致密云的星周弥散介质解释；该源代表了罕见的能驱动射电外流的变脸AGN，需继续多波段观测以揭示环境对变脸现象的调控作用。

Abstract: SDSS J1548+2208 is a unique partially-obscured nuclear transient that exhibits multiwavelength outbursts in mid-infrared, X-ray and radio. We present the results from multiwavelength photometric and spectroscopic follow-up observations with a time span of ~2500 days since its discovery. We find that the mid-infrared and X-ray emission (with a hard X-ray spectrum) are still in a high flux level relative to the pre-flare state, suggesting a sudden increased, and possibly long-sustained accreting activity from central black hole. This is supported by the slowly-evolving high-ionization coronal lines. The mid-infrared color turns blue slowly in the rising phase, which is distinct from stellar tidal disruption events (TDEs). All these properties point to the origin of outbursts from an extreme changing-look AGN and the scenario with a normal TDE seems disfavored. The radio spectral energy distribution (SED) in ~0.65-15 GHz is unusual, displaying a double-peak feature with distinct variability characteristics. In addition, we find evidence for the late-time radio rebrightening more than six years since the initial outburst, as well as a possibly new X-ray flare, though the significance for the latter is not high. The peculiar radio flux and SED evolution could be explained by a nascent outflow expanding into and shocking circumnuclear diffuse medium filled by denser clouds. In this case, SDSS J1548+2208 represents a rare changing-look AGN which can launch radio outflows. Continued multiwavelength observations are required to map the dust and gas distribution on pc-scales, providing new insights into the environmental properties that could regulate AGN changing-look phenomenon.

</details>


### [100] [Extending the infrastructure of the BAM code towards resistive general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics: tests and first applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11670)
*Matthew Beaudoin,Maximilano Ujevic,Ramon Jaeger,Anna Neuweiler,Henrique Gieg,Kenta Kiuchi,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在数值相对论代码BAM中实现了电阻性广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）框架，通过一系列基准测试验证，并应用于孤立及双中子星系统模拟。研究发现非理想效应（如平行电场分量可达总电场强度的10%）对后并合残余体的长期演化具有潜在重要性，尤其影响低密度区域的磁放大、质量抛射等过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想磁流体近似无法描述欧姆耗散和磁重联等非理想过程，而这些过程在中子星及其并合后演化中至关重要，因此需要发展电阻性GRMHD框架以超越理想近似。

Method: 在数值相对论代码BAM中扩展实现电阻性GRMHD描述，通过特殊相对论MHD基准测试进行验证，并对孤立和双中子星系统开展稳定模拟。

Result: 成功实现并验证了电阻性GRMHD框架；在双中子星模拟中发现，平行于磁场的电场分量可达到总电场强度的10%，表明非理想效应不可忽略。

Conclusion: 尽管当前研究限于简化分段多方状态方程，但新框架展示了模拟非理想GRMHD效应的能力，为未来采用更真实微观物理的研究奠定了基础，对精确建模后并合残余体长期演化具有重要意义。

Abstract: Many astrophysical phenomena, including pulsars and short gamma-ray bursts, are associated with the extremely strong magnetic fields present in neutron stars and neutron star mergers. While the ideal magnetohydrodynamic approximation, which assumes infinite conductivity, provides an excellent description of the neutron-star interior, it cannot capture non-ideal processes such as Ohmic dissipation and magnetic reconnection. To overcome this limitation, we present an extension of the numerical-relativity code BAM incorporating a resistive general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (GRMHD) description. We validate the new implementation through an extensive suite of special-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic benchmark tests and by performing stable simulations of isolated and binary neutron star systems. For the latter, we investigate the impact of finite conductivity on magnetic-field amplification, mass ejection, and non-ideal GRMHD effects. In particular, we find that the component of the electric field parallel to the magnetic field, which is zero in the ideal case, can reach up to 10% of the total electric field strength. This highlights the potential importance of non-ideal effects for accurately modeling the long-term evolution of post-merger remnants, particularly in low-density regions. Although the present study is restricted to simplified piecewise-polytropic equations of state, it demonstrates the capabilities of the new resistive GRMHD framework and paves the way for future investigations employing more realistic microphysics.

</details>


### [101] [Tidal deformation and strain accumulation of solid compact stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11780)
*Hongxiang Shen,Yong Gao,Hong-Bo Li,Ren-Xin Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于strangeon-star模型，建立了固体致密星潮汐形变的计算框架。研究发现，固体与流体strangeon星在1.4 M⊙时的潮汐形变度差异约40%，导致I-Love关系偏离约10%。在双星旋进过程中，内部应变在星体中心达到峰值；当引力波频率达到数百Hz时，大规模断裂可释放高达10^46 erg的弹性能量，足以驱动伽马射线暴的前驱体。这种固-液转变会改变潮汐响应并影响引力波波形和相位，为多信使检验脉冲星类致密星的固体性质提供了途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密星内部是流体还是固体尚未被充分检验，而两者在潮汐响应上存在显著差异。引力波观测（如GW170817）已开始约束致密物质的物态方程，但前提假设为流体内部。因此，亟需建立固体致密星的潮汐形变模型，以区分这两种可能的状态。

Method: 基于strangeon-star夸克星模型，引入剪切模量μ=10^34 erg cm^-3表征固体性质，建立固体致密星潮汐形变的理论框架。计算潮汐形变度及其与流体模型的差异，分析双星旋进过程中内部应变的累积与分布，并评估大规模断裂释放的弹性能量。

Result: 固体与流体strangeon星在1.4 M⊙时潮汐形变度相对差异约40%，导致I-Love关系偏离约10%。内部应变峰值位于星体中心，当引力波频率达数百Hz时发生大规模断裂，释放能量约10^46 erg，足以产生伽马射线暴前驱体。

Conclusion: 固体致密星的潮汐响应与流体情况存在显著差异，大规模断裂导致的固-液转变会改变潮汐形变并影响引力波波形和相位。结合前驱电磁辐射和引力波信号，固体致密星可以通过多信使观测进行检验。

Abstract: The tidal deformability of compact stars encodes the equation of state of dense matter, and gravitational-wave observations such as GW170817 have begun to constrain it under the assumption of a fluid interior. Yet whether the interior of pulsar-like compact stars is fluid or solid remains largely untested, despite the distinct tidal responses the two states predict. In this work, based on the strangeon-star model, we develop a framework for modeling tidal deformation in solid compact stars. Adopting a shear modulus of $μ= 10^{34}\,\mathrm{erg}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$, we find a relative difference of approximately $40\%$ in tidal deformability between solid and fluid strangeon stars of $1.4\,M_\odot$, corresponding to a $\sim 10\%$ deviation from the universal I--Love relation. We further model the accumulation of internal strain during binary inspiral and find that it peaks near the stellar center. When the gravitational-wave frequency reaches several hundred $\rm Hz$, large-scale fracturing occurs and can release up to $\sim 10^{46}\,\mathrm{erg}$ of elastic energy, sufficient to power short $γ$-ray-burst precursors. This solid-to-fluid transition alters the tidal response and imprints on the waveform and phase of the emitted gravitational radiation. Combined with the precursor electromagnetic emission, these gravitational-wave signatures offer a multi-messenger avenue to test the solid nature of pulsar-like compact stars.

</details>


### [102] [HAMCOR: A physics-driven Hamiltonian framework for inferring AGN coronal geometry from X-ray reverberation lags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11805)
*Buffoli Fabio*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: HAMCOR是一种几何无关的X射线日冕结构推断方法，将日冕几何反演重新定义为物理哈密顿量的基态选择，通过五个竞争性物理约束在柱坐标网格上离散化发射率分布，实现对吸积黑洞X射线日冕结构的几何无关推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模板拟合方法（如lamppost模型）在推断吸积黑洞X射线日冕几何结构时存在局限性，需要一种更灵活、几何无关的推断方法。

Method: 将日冕表示为柱坐标网格上的离散发射率分布，通过五个物理约束（磁相干性、时延一致性、照度一致性、对产生稳定性、能量预算可行性）构建哈密顿量，采用带Armijo回溯线搜索的投影梯度下降在概率单纯形上求解基态。

Result: 在三种合成几何（lamppost、柱、环）上验证，恢复空间相关性rho=0.24/0.50/0.12，分数时延误差低于24%；对5个源（4个AGN和恒星质量黑洞Cyg X-1）应用，跨7个数量级黑洞质量范围恢复一致的延展盘-日冕几何；Mrk 335多历元分析显示日冕质心稳定在(R_c,z_c)~(6.3,0.5) r_g。

Conclusion: HAMCOR能够从回响时延测量中稳健推断日冕几何结构，跨质量范围得到一致的延展盘-日冕几何，Schwarzschild-Shapiro延迟修正平均占平坦时空时延的~79%，且恢复的空间形态对该修正稳健。

Abstract: We present HAMCOR (Hamiltonian-based AGN Multi-constraint CORonal inference framework), a geometry-agnostic method for inferring the X-ray coronal structure of accreting black holes using reverberation-lag measurements. Unlike conventional template-fitting approaches, HAMCOR reframes coronal geometry inference as the ground-state selection of a physical Hamiltonian. The corona is represented as a discrete emissivity distribution over a cylindrical grid, and its geometry emerges from five competing physical constraints: magnetic coherence, lag consistency, illumination consistency, pair-production stability, and energy budget feasibility. Minimisation is performed via projected gradient descent with Armijo backtracking line search on the probability simplex. We validate HAMCOR on three synthetic geometries (lamppost, column, ring) using the same grid as the real-data fits, recovering spatial correlations rho = 0.24, 0.50, 0.12 and fractional lag errors below 24 per cent. A hyperparameter sensitivity analysis confirms robustness over more than one order of magnitude in the coupling constants. We apply HAMCOR to five sources spanning seven orders of magnitude in black hole mass: four AGN observed with XMM-Newton (Mrk 335, 1H 0707-495, IRAS 13224-3809, MCG-6-30-15) and the stellar-mass black hole binary Cyg X-1 (M_bh = 14.8 M_sun), recovering consistent extended disc-corona geometries across the full mass range. We further present a multi-epoch analysis of Mrk 335 across five XMM-Newton observations (2006-2019), revealing that the coronal centroid remains stable at (R_c, z_c) ~ (6.3, 0.5) r_g across flux states spanning a factor of ~15 in reverberation lag amplitude, arguing against a collapsing or expanding lamppost. Schwarzschild-Shapiro delay corrections amount to ~79 per cent of the flat-spacetime lag on average; the recovered spatial morphology is robust to this correction.

</details>

### [103] [A d-Electron Route to Heavy-Fermion-Like Superconductivity via Geometrical Frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09924)
*Chaoguo Wang,Hyeonhu Bae,Jiaqi Tian,Qing-Ping Ding,Yongbin Lee,Yuji Furukawa,Shannon Gould,Brianna R. Billingsley,Si Athena Chen,Matthias Frontzek,Tai Kong,Binghai Yan,Sheng Ran,Xin Gui*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了Mo4PtGa17，一种非中心对称的d电子超导体，具有几何阻挫的呼吸烧绿石Mo晶格，通过热力学、输运和NMR测量发现其表现出重费米子行为的超导性和增强的铁磁自旋涨落，理论计算表明Mo-4d态衍生的近平带、范霍夫奇点和Kramers节线是产生重费米子行为的关键，揭示了通过几何阻挫实现d电子材料中重费米子类超导电性的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重费米子超导体主要存在于f电子材料（Kondo格子）中，而已知的d电子重费米子类系统往往与轨道选择局域矩、Hund金属物理或电荷密度波机制相关。本文旨在探索通过几何阻挫在d电子材料中实现重费米子类超导电性的新途径。

Method: 采用热力学、输运和核磁共振(NMR)测量表征超导和自旋涨落行为，结合理论计算分析电子结构，包括能带结构、范霍夫奇点和Kramers节线。

Result: Mo4PtGa17在铁磁不稳定性附近表现出重费米子类超导电性和增强的铁磁自旋涨落；Mo-4d态本征地产生近平带、范霍夫奇点和Kramers节线，且对在位电子关联具有鲁棒性；几何阻挫晶格增强了态密度、自旋磁化率和准粒子质量。

Conclusion: Mo4PtGa17通过几何阻挫机制在d电子材料中实现了重费米子类超导电性，为理解从关联自旋涨落态涌现的超导电性提供了新的研究平台。

Abstract: Heavy-fermion superconductors are mostly associated with f-electron materials with Kondo lattices, while known d-electron heavy-fermion-like systems are often linked to orbital-selective local moments, Hund-metal physics, or a charge-density-wave mechanism. Here we report Mo4PtGa17, a noncentrosymmetric itinerant d-electron superconductor with a geometrically frustrated breathing-pyrochlore Mo lattice. Thermodynamic, transport and NMR measurements reveal heavy-fermion-like behavior superconductivity and enhanced ferromagnetic spin fluctuations near a ferromagnetic instability. Theoretical calculations identify nearly flat bands, van Hove singularities and Kramers nodal lines near the Fermi energy, derived intrinsically from Mo-4d states and are robust against on-site electronic correlations. These results suggest that the geometrically frustrated lattice in Mo4PtGa17 generates an intriguing electronic structure that enhances the density of states, spin susceptibility and quasiparticle mass. Mo4PtGa17 therefore identifies a unique route to heavy-fermion-like superconductivity in d-electron materials through geometrical frustration. It also allows further investigations into superconductivity emerging from a correlated and spin-fluctuating state.

</details>


### [104] [Anyons and Inherently Complex F-symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10181)
*Matthew Buican,Peter Huston,Jiannis K. Pachos*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发现并证明了某些任意子模型的F-符号无法被表示为实数（即“固有复F-符号”），通过展示Rep(Z₇⋊Z₃)和Rep(Z₅⋊Z₄)等编织融合范畴的反例，表明这一现象与缺乏电荷共轭对称性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然常见的任意子模型（如阿贝尔任意子、Fibonacci和Ising任意子）的F-符号可被取为实数，但这一性质是否具有普遍性尚不清楚。作者旨在探究是否存在无法被实化的F-符号，从而揭示任意子模型算术结构的更深层特性。

Method: 通过分析编织融合范畴中的F-符号代数结构，作者考察了缺乏电荷共轭对称性的最小秩范畴，具体研究了Rep(Z₇⋊Z₃)和Rep(Z₅⋊Z₄)及其中心化范畴Z(Rep(Z₇⋊Z₃))和Z(Rep(Z₅⋊Z₄))，展示其F-符号无法通过基变换变为实数。

Result: 发现Rep(Z₇⋊Z₃)和Rep(Z₅⋊Z₄)及其对应的任意子模型具有固有复F-符号，提供了F-符号无法实化的具体反例。

Conclusion: 固有复F-符号的存在性与电荷共轭对称性的缺失一致，这支持了伴生工作中关于实F-符号与电荷共轭对称性等价性的逆命题。该结果对超越模数据分类任意子模型的研究具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Anyons in $2+1$ dimensions are not only characterized by exotic braiding statistics but also by intricate fusion properties. Two anyons may fuse into multiple topological charge sectors, and associativity of fusing three anyons to produce a fixed charge sector is governed by $F$-symbols. While braiding invariants, such as the modular data, are typically complex valued, a complete description of general anyon models requires understanding the arithmetic properties of its fusion associativity data as well. The $F$-symbols for many of the most common $2+1$d topological orders, including all Abelian anyon models as well as Fibonacci and Ising anyons, can be made real valued. We show this phenomenon is not universal by exhibiting braided fusion categories whose $F$-symbols cannot be made real. We call such $F$-symbols \lq\lq inherently complex." The examples we study lack a charge-conjugation symmetry and our results are therefore consistent with the converse of a statement proved in a companion work linking real $F$-symbols in braided fusion categories with the existence of a suitable charge-conjugation symmetry. We analyse the smallest-rank braided fusion categories we know of with inherently complex $F$-symbols: ${\rm Rep}(\mathbb{Z}_7\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_3)$ and ${\rm Rep}(\mathbb{Z}_5\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_4)$. Consequently, the corresponding $\mathcal Z({\rm Rep}(\mathbb{Z}_7\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_3))$ and $\mathcal Z({\rm Rep}(\mathbb{Z}_5\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_4))$ anyon models also have inherently complex $F$-symbols. Our presentation connects these examples with recent results on classifying anyons beyond modular data.

</details>


### [105] [Pinwheel-shaped bound triplet pairs in the magnetization plateaus of SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10877)
*Igor Vinograd,Philippe Corboz,Steffen Krämer,Yanan Li,Frédéric Mila,Hiroshi Kageyama,Masashi Takigawa,Mladen Horvatić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过核磁共振实验证实，SrCu₂(BO₃)₂中的低磁化平台是由自旋-2束缚态形成的维格纳晶体稳定化，而非传统构型（半经典上下或三重态晶体），从而为磁化平台理论提供了全新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SrCu₂(BO₃)₂中观测到的1/8、2/15、1/6、1/4、1/3、2/5和1/2饱和磁化平台的神秘起源问题，验证张量网络数值模拟提出的"低磁化平台是自旋-2束缚态维格纳晶体"这一新假说。

Method: 在高达41.9 T的磁场下对SrCu₂(BO₃)₂进行63,65Cu核磁共振谱学测量；基于1/3平台实验约束，比较1/8和2/15平台的实验谱与自旋-2束缚态维格纳晶体理论预测及三重态晶体模型预测。

Result: 1/8和2/15平台的核磁共振谱与自旋-2束缚态维格纳晶体预测一致，而与三重态晶体模型不兼容。

Conclusion: 证实了自旋-2束缚态维格纳晶体是稳定低磁化平台的新机制，将束缚态维格纳晶体的形成确立为磁化平台理论中的一种基础性新范式。

Abstract: The sequence of magnetization plateaus at 1/8, 2/15, 1/6, 1/4, 1/3, 2/5, and 1/2 of the saturation observed in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ remained a puzzle until tensor-networks-based numerical simulations suggested that the low-magnetization plateaus are stabilized as Wigner crystals of spin-2 bound states, and not as one of the standard configurations (semiclassical up-down, or crystals of triplets). We report on $^{63,65}$Cu nuclear magnetic resonance spectra up to 41.9 T: Based on constraints deduced from the 1/3 plateau, we show that the spectra in the 1/8 and 2/15 plateaus indeed agree with the prediction for the spin-2 bound states, while being incompatible with a crystal of triplets. This adds the formation of Wigner crystals of bound states as an alternative fundamental paradigm in the theory of magnetization plateaus.

</details>


### [106] [Generalized Keldysh formalism for nonequilibrium correlation functions and its application to fluctuation dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11055)
*Ken Inayoshi,Hiroshi Shinaoka,Yuta Murakami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在广义Keldysh形式论中引入等高线依赖虚拟探针场的方法，结合矩阵自由Krylov求解器和量子张量列车，高效计算非平衡两粒子关联函数（含顶点修正），并应用于非平衡DMFT，揭示了序参量涨落动力学对顶点修正的显著依赖性及近非热临界点处的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分辨光谱学的发展使得实验上可观测关联量子材料中的集体动力学，但理论上计算对应的非平衡两粒子关联函数（尤其是包含顶点修正）仍面临重大挑战。

Method: 在广义Keldysh形式论中引入等高线依赖的虚拟探针场，推导线性积分方程避免显式构造四时顶点核，并开发基于量子张量列车（quantics tensor trains）的矩阵自由Krylov求解器进行高效数值求解。

Result: 结合非平衡DMFT计算表明，非平衡对称破缺态中序参量的涨落动力学在是否包含顶点修正时存在显著差异；且涨落及其衰减时间在接近非热临界点时增大。

Conclusion: 该方法为计算非平衡关联函数提供了一条实用路径，这类函数正成为表征远离平衡态的关键可观测量。

Abstract: Recent advances in time-resolved spectroscopies provide increasing access to collective dynamics in correlated quantum materials. However, computing the corresponding nonequilibrium two-particle correlation functions remains a major challenge. Here, by introducing a contour-dependent virtual probe field within the generalized Keldysh formalism, we propose an approach that computes such correlation functions with the vertex corrections essential for describing collective dynamics. In particular, we introduce a linear integral equation that computes the correlation functions without explicitly constructing the four-time vertex kernel, and develop its matrix-free Krylov solver based on quantics tensor trains. Combining our method with nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory, we show that the fluctuation dynamics of the order parameter in a nonequilibrium symmetry-broken state depends significantly on whether vertex corrections are included, and that the fluctuation and its decay time grow near the nonthermal critical point. Our approach thus provides a practical route for evaluating nonequilibrium correlation functions, which are emerging as key observables for characterizing states far from equilibrium.

</details>


### [107] [Conformal Nature of Quantum Phase Transitions via Fuzzy Three-Sphere Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11371)
*Xue Meng,Liangdong Hu,Wei Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过将模糊二维球面的方法推广到高维，在(3+1)维量子临界点（等效于经典四维系统）上利用朗道能级投影到模糊三维球面，通过计算临界能量谱明确验证了态-算符对应，从而揭示了伊辛和杨-李普适类量子相变中的共形对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接获取共形代数并在微观上揭示涌现的共形对称性（尤其是在高维中）仍然是一个重大挑战，本文旨在推广模糊二维球面的方法到更高维度，以揭示(3+1)维量子临界点处的共形性。

Method: 将模糊二维球面的CFT特征揭示方法推广到高维，通过朗道能级投影到模糊三维球面，构建(3+1)维量子模型（等效于经典四维系统），研究伊辛和杨-李普适类的量子相变，并计算临界能量谱以验证态-算符对应。

Result: 通过计算临界能量谱，明确验证了态-算符对应（共形不变性的标志），在伊辛和杨-李普适类量子相变中成功暴露了共形对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作结合先前进展，为高维相变中涌现共形性的微观研究建立了新途径。

Abstract: Conformal field theory (CFT) offers a modern viewpoint for understanding phase transitions. However, directly accessing the conformal algebra and microscopically uncovering the emergent conformal symmetry, especially in higher dimensions, remains a significant challenge. Motivated by recent advances in revealing CFT features via the fuzzy two-sphere, here we generalize this approach to higher dimensions and aim to expose the conformality at the (3+1)-D quantum critical point. We demonstrate this framework by investigating quantum phase transitions belonging to the Ising and Yang-Lee universality classes in a (3+1)-D quantum model (equivalent to a classical four-dimensional system), realized via Landau level projection on the fuzzy three-sphere. By computing the energy spectra at criticality, we explicitly verify the state-operator correspondence, a hallmark of conformal invariance. Together with prior advances, this work establishes a new pathway for the microscopic study of emergent conformality in higher-dimensional phase transitions.

</details>


### [108] [Fractionalized metals from doped anyons: Application to tMoTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11484)
*T. Senthil*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在掺杂σ_xy=2e^2/3h分数量子反常霍尔态时，高电阻率金属态是Z₃正交金属——一种由电荷1/3费米子准粒子和Z₃规范场耦合而成的非费米液体，并讨论了其与超导态的关联及实验特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受扭曲MoTe₂近期实验启发，解释在掺杂2/3分数量子反常霍尔态时观察到的高电阻率金属态的本质。

Method: 构造两种U(3)对称的Z₃正交金属态，区分无间隙费米子作为SU(3)三重态或单态；分析电荷1/3任意子的配对机制。

Result: Z₃正交金属即使在分数化准粒子处于良好金属态时也能产生高电阻率；电荷1/3费米子配对可得到普通电荷2e超导体，与BCS态平滑连接。

Conclusion: 轻掺杂2/3态的金属正常态可能具有分数化载流子，为理解FQAH态附近的金属行为提供了新框架。

Abstract: A fluid of mobile anyons may arise naturally when a Fractional Quantum Anomalous Hall (FQAH) state is doped. Motivated by recent experiments on twisted $MoTe_2$, we study metallic phases obtained by doping the $σ_{xy} = 2e^2/3h$ state. We propose that the high-resistivity metal observed adjacent to this FQAH state is a $Z_3$ Orthogonal Metal: a non-Fermi liquid with sharp charge $1/3$ fermionic quasiparticles coupled to a discrete $Z_3$ gauge field but with no sharp electronic quasiparticle. For a dilute gas of three species of charge-$1/3$ anyons with $π/3$ statistics, we construct two $U(3)$ symmetric $Z_3$ Orthogonal Metals, distinguished by whether the gapless fermions transform as $SU(3)$ triplets or singlets. We show that these states naturally yield large electrical resistivities even when the fractionalized quasiparticles are in a good metallic regime. Pairing of the charge-$1/3$ fermions produces an ordinary charge-$2e$ superconductor, smoothly connected to a BCS state but obtained through an intrinsically fractionalized normal state. We discuss experimental signatures of the idea that the normal metallic state in the lightly doped $2/3$ state may have fractionalized charge carriers.

</details>


### [109] [Stripe-Ordered Altermagnetism Emerging from Correlation-Driven Spin-Density-Wave Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11532)
*Zenghui Fan,Jingyao Meng,Tianxing Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过哈特里-福克平均场和行列式量子蒙特卡洛方法，在铁基超导相关的最小哈伯德模型中揭示了由自旋密度波不稳定性驱动的dxy波条纹有序交变磁绝缘相，该相在有限温度下稳定存在，突破了传统共线反铁磁的交变磁范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁通常在共线反铁磁范式中被识别，但它在其他自旋不稳定性（如自旋密度波）中是否可能实现仍是一个根本性的开放问题，作者试图探索这一可能性。

Method: 结合哈特里-福克平均场理论和无偏的行列式量子蒙特卡洛方法，研究铁基超导相关的最小哈伯德模型。

Result: 发现了一种由(π,0) SDW不稳定性驱动的新型dxy波条纹有序交变磁绝缘相；引入的单轴交错电势破缺了Td𝒯对称性而保留了M𝒯对称性，从而解锁了显著的非相对论自旋分裂；DQC有限尺寸标度证实该相在有限温度下稳定存在。

Conclusion: 将交变磁的边界从传统反铁磁范式推进到自旋密度波不稳定性领域，增进了对强关联电子系统中交变磁的基本理解。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is conventionally identified within the paradigm of collinear antiferromagnets. Its potential realization within other spin instabilities, such as a spin-density wave (SDW), remains a fundamentally compelling open question. Here, we combine Hartree-Fock mean-field and unbiased determinant quantum Monte Carlo methods to investigate a minimal Hubbard model relevant to iron pnictides. We reveal a novel $d_{xy}$-wave stripe-ordered altermagnetic (SOAM) insulating phase driven fundamentally by the correlation-induced $(π,0)$ SDW instability. Within this phase, an introduced uniaxial staggered electric potential alters the underlying symmetry: it breaks the original combined time-reversal and spatial translation symmetry ($T_{d}\mathcal{T}$) and retains a combined time-reversal and mirror invariance ($M\mathcal{T}$), thereby unlocking the pronounced nonrelativistic spin splitting. Crucially, the exact finite-size scaling from our determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations confirms that this correlation-driven SOAM phase stably survives at accessible finite temperatures. Our study pushes the frontier of altermagnetism beyond the conventional antiferromagnetic paradigm into the realm of SDW instability, advancing the fundamental understanding of altermagnetism in strongly correlated electron systems.

</details>


### [110] [Electronic tuning of the soft-phonon transport anomaly in Ta$_2$Ni(S$_x$Se$_{1-x}$)$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11679)
*Yuan-Shan Zhang,Masahiko Isobe,Hidenori Takagi,Dennis Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过硫替换调控能带重叠/能隙，揭示了Ta₂Ni(S_xSe_{1-x})₅中软声子输运异常与电子不稳定性之间的紧密关联：在富硒端，电子不稳定性增强相变温度并放大实验信号，表现为伴随电子涨落的软声子输运异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Ta₂NiSe₅在T_c=326 K处热输运中巨大各向异性声子散射（与横向声学声子软化相关）的微观起源及其物理意义。

Method: 通过硫替换（S substitution）调控正常态的能带重叠/能隙，系统研究Ta₂Ni(S_xSe_{1-x})₅系列材料的热输运和相变行为。

Result: 当硫含量升高、体系趋近能带绝缘体时，电子跃迁特征被抑制，软声子输运异常随之消失；富硒端电子不稳定性增强T_c至326 K并放大实验信号。

Conclusion: 该材料家族存在两种机制：富硫端仅晶格不稳定性驱动弱结构相变（T_c~130 K）；富硒端额外的电子不稳定性显著增强相变，强电子-空穴-晶格相互作用表现为伴随激子和杂化能隙涨落的软声子输运异常。

Abstract: Ta$_2$NiSe$_5$ continues to be investigated for its phase transition at $T_\textrm{c}$ = 326 K, where it develops both an electronic gap and a distortion of its Ta/Ni chains. One intriguing feature at $T_\textrm{c}$ seen in thermal transport is the giant anisotropic scattering of phonons moving perpendicular to the chains, which is apparently associated with the softening of a transverse acoustic phonon, but whose microscopic origin and significance demand clarification. By tuning the normal-state band overlap/gap with S substitution, we uncover a close connection between this soft-phonon transport anomaly and underlying electronic instabilities: When Ta$_2$Ni(S$_x$Se$_{1-x}$)$_5$ approaches a band insulator at high $x$, and signatures of the electronic transition are suppressed, the soft-phonon transport anomaly concomitantly vanishes. Our results establish the following picture for the Ta$_2$Ni(S$_x$Se$_{1-x}$)$_5$ family: Near the S end, a sole lattice instability gives rise to a weak structural transition with $T_\textrm{c}$ approaching 130 K. Near the Se end, additional electronic instabilities boost $T_\textrm{c}$ up to 326 K and amplify experimental signatures of the transition. The strong interaction between electrons, holes, and the lattice is manifested as a soft-phonon transport anomaly accompanied by electronic fluctuations, which include excitonic and hybridization-gap fluctuations.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [The Dirac equation in (split-)octonions: origins, variants, and modern context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09703)
*J. Köplinger*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该综述论文系统梳理了八元数与分裂八元数在狄拉克方程中的四种不同表示方法：2因子（直积）、3因子、投影表示（本征使用八元数基元建模时空基），以及传统上使用八元数承载狄拉克代数作用于旋量。论文特别追溯了2因子方法的起源（Köplinger, 2006，基于Musès猜想），并着重分析了Gogberashvili与Gurchumelia提出的"分裂八元数狄拉克方程"，通过显式保结构旋转证明其实质上等同于直积表示（仅重新标记），指出其独立验证价值而非"新颖形式"所声称的创新性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 八元数在物理中的应用存在多种概念上不同的表示方式，容易造成混淆。本文旨在系统性地对这些表示进行分类整理，澄清它们之间的数学关系，并特别纠正近期文献中关于"分裂八元数狄拉克方程"声称的创新性认知。

Method: 采用文献综述与数学分析相结合的方法：首先按表示结构将相关文献分为四类（2因子、3因子、投影表示、传统表示）；然后通过显式的保结构旋转（structure-preserving rotation）数学推导，证明Gogberashvili与Gurchumelia的"分裂八元数狄拉克方程"在重新标记后等价于Köplinger的直积表示。

Result: 识别并分类了八元数表示狄拉克方程的四种不同途径；确认2因子表示源于Köplinger（2006）对Musès猜想的实现；证明了Gogberashvili与Gurchumelia声称的"新颖形式"实际上是直积表示的重新参数化，其真正贡献在于为已知表示提供了独立验证，而非提出了全新形式。

Conclusion: 八元数在狄拉克方程中的应用存在多种不同表示，需要谨慎区分。近期文献中声称的"新颖形式"在数学结构上等价于已有表示，但仍具有独立的验证价值。该综述为理解八元数物理应用提供了清晰的分类框架。

Abstract: Octonions and split-octonions have been used to express the Dirac equation in physics in several conceptually distinct ways. This review sorts them into four groups: the 2-factor (direct-product), 3-factor, and projection representations, which use (split-)octonion basis elements natively to model a spacetime basis; and the conventional use of octonions to carry Dirac algebra acting on spinors. Pointers to their modern use, and to recent methods for native split-octonionic analysis, are given throughout. Within the 2-factor approach, we document its origin (J. Köplinger, Appl. Math. Computation 182 (2006) 443, following a conjecture of C. Musès). As a side aspect, we examine the "split octonionic Dirac equation" of M. Gogberashvili and A. Gurchumelia (Int. J. Geom. Meth. Mod. Phys. 21 (2024) 2450214). An explicit structure-preserving rotation shows it to be this same direct-product representation, up to relabeling: an independent verification rather than "the novel forms" [the opening words of the authors' abstract]. Its genuine contributions are credited.

</details>


### [112] [PBW bases and centralisers for the $q$-Onsager algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10097)
*Haoran Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于q-Onsager代数的PBW基和中心化子的五个猜想。证明了Baseilhac-Kolb根向量在q非单位根时构成PBW基，建立了12种交替生成元PBW基并证明其在任意标量中心特化下保持，确定了负交替子代数和虚交替子代数的中心化子，并证明了所有四个单族交替多项式子代数是极大交换的。证明方法结合了Damiani根向量的显式 straightened、退化gr O_q ≃ U_q^+(widehat{sl}_2)和大指标三角交叉论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决q-Onsager代数中关于PBW基的存在性和结构、中心化子的确定等五个长期未解决的猜想，这些猜想由Terwilliger、Baseilhac和Belliard等人提出，对于理解Onsager代数及其在可积系统、统计力学中的表示理论具有重要意义。

Method: 采用三种关键技术：(1) Damiani根向量的显式 straightened 技术，(2) 退化 gr O_q ≃ U_q^+(widehat{sl}_2) 的同构关系，(3) 大指标三角交叉论证方法。

Result: (1) 去除了q非单位根时PBW基存在的超越性假设条件；(2) 建立了12种交替生成元PBW基并在任意标量中心特化下保持；(3) 确定了负交替子代数和虚交替子代数的中心化子；(4) 证明了四个单族交替多项式子代数是极大交换的。

Conclusion: 完整解决了q-Onsager代数结构理论中的五个核心猜想，为后续表示论和可积系统的研究提供了坚实的代数基础。

Abstract: We settle five conjectures concerning PBW bases and centralisers for the $q$-Onsager algebra. We first prove that the Baseilhac--Kolb root vectors give a PBW basis in every linear order whenever $q$ is not a root of unity, removing the previous transcendence hypothesis. We next establish twelve PBW bases in the alternating generators and show that they persist under arbitrary scalar central specialisation of the alternating central extension. This proves conjectures of Terwilliger and of Baseilhac and Belliard. Finally, we determine the centralisers of the negative and imaginary alternating subalgebras and show that all four single-family alternating polynomial subalgebras are maximal commutative. The proofs combine explicit straightening for Damiani root vectors, the degeneration $\mathrm{gr} O_q \simeq U_q^+(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}}_2)$, and large-index triangular crossing arguments.

</details>


### [113] [The existence of full dimensional KAM tori for infinite dimensional Hamiltonian systems with long range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10259)
*Hongzi Cong,Wanran Ding,Zhihan Zhang,Juan Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在Bourgain型丢番图条件下，具有长程相互作用的无限维力学系统存在满维KAM环面，且不变环面半径满足更慢的衰减速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将KAM理论推广到具有长程相互作用的无限维力学系统，放宽对不变环面半径衰减速率的要求。

Method: 采用Bourgain型丢番图条件，通过分析长程相互作用下系统的结构，证明满维KAM环面的存在性。

Result: 证明了在更弱的衰减条件下，具有长程相互作用的无限维力学系统仍存在满维KAM环面。

Conclusion: Bourgain型丢番图条件适用于长程相互作用系统，且不变环面半径的衰减速率可以比传统条件更慢。

Abstract: We prove the existence of full dimensional KAM tori for infinite dimensional mechanical systems exhibiting long range interactions, under a Diophantine condition of Bourgain type [Bourgain2005JFA], in which the radius of the invariant tori satisfies a slower decay.

</details>


### [114] [Path-Dependent Entropic Lagrangian for Probability Flows: Balance--Entropy Routing and Composable Information Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10493)
*Huilong Ren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路径依赖的熵拉格朗日微积分，将静态最大熵状态选择扩展为概率路径演化，通过受限生成元、上极限历史项和显式平衡-熵端口路由，建立热力学状态关系、保守概率平衡和非负生产，并数值验证了质量守恒、能量分解和自由能核算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵方法仅指定约束下的概率状态，但未说明状态如何达到、概率如何传输、路径上的耗散和外部信息交换如何核算，缺乏对概率路径演化的动力学描述。

Method: 提出路径依赖的熵拉格朗日微积分，引入受限生成元、上极限历史项和显式平衡-熵端口路由，构建热力学状态关系、保守概率平衡和非负生产，在KL/香农扇区恢复最大熵和贝叶斯定律为稳态无通量状态，可组合信息势和结构势控制尾部、稀疏性、鲁棒性、正则化和非局部多模态。

Result: 两个数值示例验证了质量守恒、能量分解和总自由能核算的正确性；该方法在KL/香农扇区可恢复最大熵和贝叶斯定律作为稳态解。

Conclusion: 所提出的路径依赖熵拉格朗日微积分框架能够统一描述概率路径演化中的耗散与信息交换，为概率学习提供了更完备的理论基础。

Abstract: Probability distributions are central to information theory, statistical inference, and modern probabilistic learning. Maximum entropy selects a probability state under prescribed constraints, but it does not specify how that state is reached, how probability is transported, or how dissipation and external information exchange are accounted for along the path. We develop a path-dependent entropic Lagrangian calculus that extends static state selection to probability-path evolution through restricted generators, upper-limit history terms, and explicit balance--entropy port routing. The construction yields the thermal state relation, conservative probability balance, and nonnegative production under standard mobility closure. Its KL/Shannon sector recovers maximum-entropy and Bayesian laws as stationary no-flux states, while time-dependent information potentials separate internal dissipation from supplied information power. Composable information and structural potentials control tails, sparsity, robustness, regularization, and nonlocal multimodality without changing the accounting architecture. Two numerical examples verify mass conservation, energy decomposition, and the total free-energy ledger.

</details>


### [115] [Rigorous bound on the aspect ratio for the formation of a nematic phase in hard rod and hard rectangle systems on $\mathbb{Z}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10510)
*Qidong He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明二维方格上长宽比 $k=l/w$ 足够大（至少 $10^{72}$）的硬矩形模型存在向列相，基于双尺度簇展开方法，给出了首个严格的相变所需长宽比估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为硬矩形系统形成向列相所需的长宽比提供首个严格数学证明，填补该领域缺少严格定量估计的空白。

Method: 采用 Disertori--Giuliani 等人之前发展的双尺度簇展开方法，通过显式追踪常量和参数，补全之前工作中隐含的论证步骤，推导出充分定量条件并提取所需长宽比的下界估计。

Result: 得到向列相存在的充分条件，非优化评估给出 $k\ge 10^{72}$ 的界，虽然远大于数值预测的 $k_{\min}=7$，但这是首个严格的解析估计。

Conclusion: 证明了硬矩形模型中向列相存在的严格性，为后续改进提供了定量框架。

Abstract: We prove the existence of a nematic phase in a model of $l\times w$ hard rectangles on the square lattice with two allowed orientations and a large aspect ratio $k:=l/w$. The proof is based on a two-scale cluster expansion method developed previously by Disertori--Giuliani for hard rods in 2D and Disertori--Giuliani--Jauslin for hard plates in 3D. Our main contributions lie in explicitly tracking the constants and parameters and completing the arguments left implicit in these works. Hence, the proof produces a sufficient set of quantitative conditions from which estimates for the required aspect ratio can be extracted. A non-optimized evaluation of these conditions yields the bound $k\ge 10^{72}$. Although it vastly overshoots the numerical prediction, $k_{\min}=7$, our result appears to be the first rigorous estimate of the aspect ratio required for the formation of a nematic phase in hard rectangle systems.

</details>


### [116] [Jordan Pair Quantum Theory and the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10833)
*John C. Baez,Endre Bokor,Latham Boyle*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现双例外埃尔米特Jordan三元组（“双Cayley”三元组）与粒子物理标准模型结构之间存在惊人对应关系，并展示了如何使用埃尔米特Jordan三元组重构和推广普通量子力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家从研究描述量子力学可观测量的Jordan代数中发现了Jordan对和埃尔米特Jordan三元组，本文旨在揭示这些数学结构与粒子物理标准模型之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过分析双例外埃尔米特Jordan三元组（bi-Cayley triple）的数学性质，将其与标准模型的粒子结构进行对应比较，并探索用埃尔米特Jordan三元组重新表述量子力学的可能性。

Result: 发现双例外埃尔米特Jordan三元组与标准模型结构之间存在惊人的对应关系；同时证明了普通量子力学可以用埃尔米特Jordan三元组进行重新表述和推广。

Conclusion: 数学中的埃尔米特Jordan三元组，特别是双例外情况，为理解粒子物理标准模型提供了新的视角，并且为量子力学的推广提供了数学框架。

Abstract: Jordan pairs and hermitian Jordan triples were discovered by mathematicians studying Jordan algebras, which describe the possible algebras of observables in quantum mechanics. We point out a striking correspondence between the doubly exceptional hermitian Jordan triple (the so-called ``bi-Cayley'' triple) and the structure of the Standard Model of particle physics. We also point out how ordinary quantum mechanics may be reformulated, and generalized, using hermitian Jordan triples.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Critical Slowing Down: Slow Modes, Extreme Tails, and Field Decoherence in Tipping Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11350)
*Mickaël D. Chekroun,Valerio Lucarini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究气候临界点的早期预警信号，利用随机Ghil-Sellers能量平衡模型，通过三种互补视角（约化Ruelle-Pollicott共振、极值统计、全场地适应谐波模态）分析亚稳态临界转变，揭示了整体弛豫、尾部极端事件和空间相位组织三个相互作用的方面在临界点附近的变化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单一标量指标难以全面捕捉气候临界点前的复杂动态变化，需要多视角、多层次的预警方法来区分临界转变的不同方面（整体弛豫、极端事件尾部、空间相位组织），从而更可靠地识别早期预警信号。

Method: 采用三种互补方法：1) 构建全球平均温度与经向热对比的约化转移算子，估计约化RP共振和Kolmogorov模态；2) 应用极值理论分析全球平均温度异常的冷尾部形状参数和极值指数；3) 利用数据适应谐波模态分析全温度场的多变量相位相干性。

Result: 1) 临界点附近，多个主导衰减率下降，模态沿共同慢方向协调，格林函数出现相干延迟恢复尾部和低频敏感性增强；2) 冷尾部边界变模糊且更持久，形状和极值指数呈现不对称组织；3) 全场地适应谐波模态相位分布变宽，表明多变量相位退相干。

Conclusion: 亚稳态临界转变的特征是约化谱响应、极端事件统计和全场相位相干性的联合重组，预警信号由一束慢模态而非单一谱间隙承载。

Abstract: We study early-warning signals of climate tipping in the metastable stochastic Ghil--Sellers energy balance model. Rather than relying on a single scalar indicator, we analyze the transition through three complementary lenses: reduced Ruelle--Pollicott (RP) resonances, extreme value statistics, and full-field data-adaptive harmonic modes. This distinguishes bulk relaxation, tail excursions, and spatial phase organization as interacting aspects of tipping. First, using a reduced transfer-operator construction for global mean temperature and meridional thermal contrast, we estimate reduced RP resonances and Kolmogorov modes. Near tipping, several dominant decay rates drop and their modes harmonize along a common slow direction. Consequently, Green's functions aligned with this direction acquire coherent delayed-recovery tails and enhanced low-frequency susceptibility. The warning is thus carried by a bundle of slow modes rather than a single spectral gap. Second, Extreme Value Theory reveals that the cold tail of the global mean temperature anomaly becomes less sharply bounded and more persistent near the transition. The shape and extremal indices show an asymmetric organization: cold excursions probing the escape direction become more accessible and clustered. Third, Data-Adaptive Harmonic Mode (DAHM) analysis of the full temperature field shows that near tipping, leading modes still capture the large-scale trend, but fixed-rank reconstruction degrades and the DAHM phase distribution broadens. We interpret this as multivariate phase decoherence: the field retains a coherent transition component while losing sharp latitudinal phase organization. Ultimately, metastable tipping is marked by a joint reorganization of reduced spectral response, extreme-event statistics, and full-field phase coherence.

</details>


### [118] [Correlated and uncorrelated long--time asymptotics of type D ASEP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11376)
*Jeffrey Kuan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究D型ASEP（双物种不对称排斥过程）的长时渐近行为，发现在固定q和弱不对称两种机制下，两个物种的密度场和涨落均呈现退耦现象，但极限正态随机变量之间存在非平凡的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解D型ASEP这一双物种相互作用粒子系统的长期动力学行为，特别是两个保守物种之间耦合与退耦的精确机制。

Method: 利用精确流退耦恒等式分析固定q区域的流体力学极限和Tracy-Widom涨落；在弱不对称（Edwards-Wilkinson）机制下分析密度涨落场的渐近行为，计算极限相关函数。

Result: 固定q区域：流体力学极限和Tracy-Widom涨落退耦；弱不对称区域：两个密度涨落场退耦为线性随机热方程，无漂移或噪声交叉耦合，但极限正态随机变量之间存在关联，相关系数为(1-e^{-4c})/(4c)，正部关联由Bessel-Struve函数表达。

Conclusion: D型ASEP的两种物种在渐近水平上表现出非平凡的退耦-关联并存现象：动力学退耦但极限统计量存在新关联函数。

Abstract: The type D ASEP is an asymmetric two--species interacting particle system on $\Z$, in which two separately conserved species hop, bind into a composite ``bound pair'', and split. The model, along with its reversible measures and orthogonal polynomial duality, was constructed using the representation theory of $U_q(\so_{2n})$. The reversible measures and orthogonal polynomial duality are each a product of two copies of the single-species ASEP reversible measures and orthogonal polynomial duality. In this paper, we study the long-time asymptotics of the type D ASEP.
  In the fixed--$q$ regime, using an exact current--decoupling identity, we prove that the asymptotic hydrodynamic limit and Tracy--Widom fluctuations decouple, as predicted from the duality. In the weak--asymmetry (Edwards--Wilkinson) regime, when $q=1-c/N^2$, we prove that the two density fluctuation fields \underline{decouple}: each converges to a linear stochastic heat equation, with no cross--coupling in either the drift or the noise, the limiting noises having vanishing cross--correlation. More surprisingly, we then prove that the two limiting normal random variables are \underline{correlated} with a seemingly new correlation function. The correlation is exactly equal to $(1-e^{-4c})/(4c)$, with the positive parts of the normal random variables having correlations expressed by the Bessel--Struve function.
  This paper, with the exception of the abstract and introduction, was written entirely by Claude Opus 4.8 and Fable 5. The proofs were then formalized in Lean, using Aristotle by Harmonic AI. The human author of this paper verified the proofs manually.

</details>


### [119] [On The Eigenvalue Rigidity of the Laguerre Unitary Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11547)
*Chenhao Lu,Xiaolu Yue*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Laguerre酉系综特征值的最优全局刚性估计，通过构造随机测度并证明其收敛到高斯乘性混沌测度，从而得到刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Laguerre酉系综特征值的最优全局刚性估计，这是随机矩阵理论中的一个重要问题，涉及特征值在宏观尺度上的涨落行为。

Method: 利用中心极限定理，通过特征值计数函数构造一个随机测度；然后应用Claeys等人[7]的充分条件，对相应的指数矩进行渐近分析，证明该随机测度收敛到高斯乘性混沌测度。

Result: 证明了构造的随机测度收敛到高斯乘性混沌测度，从而得到了Laguerre酉系综特征值的最优全局刚性估计。

Conclusion: 通过随机测度的收敛性建立了最优全局刚性估计，为理解Laguerre酉系综特征值的宏观涨落提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an optimal global rigidity estimate for the eigenvalues of the Laguerre unitary ensemble. Using the central limit theorem, we first construct a random measure via the eigenvalue counting function and then prove its convergence to a Gaussian multiplicative chaos measure, which yields the desired rigidity result. To prove this convergence, we apply a sufficient condition due to Claeys et al. [7] and carry out an asymptotic analysis of the corresponding exponential moments.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Weights and characters for affine Hecke algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10010)
*Maximilien J. Mackie*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了仿射Hecke代数中权（weights）对单模（simple modules）分类的决定作用，并利用这种关系描述了沿同源（isogenies）的归纳（induction）与限制（restriction）操作，同时讨论了厄米特对偶（hermitian duals）和幺正性（unitarity）在这些操作下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权在仿射Hecke代数的单模分类中发挥关键作用，但权决定单模的程度尚不明确，且沿同源的归纳与限制操作缺乏系统描述。

Method: 通过分析权对单模的决定程度，建立沿同源的归纳与限制函子；在此基础上进一步研究厄米特对偶和幺正性在这些函子下的变换规律。

Result: 明确了权确定单模的限度，并给出了沿同源的归纳与限制操作的具体描述；同时获得了厄米特对偶和幺正性在这些操作下的行为特征。

Conclusion: 权是仿射Hecke代数单模分类的核心要素，沿同源的归纳与限制为理解模结构提供了有效工具，且厄米特对偶与幺正性在该框架下具有良好的可传递性。

Abstract: Weights play an essential role in the classification of simple modules for affine Hecke algebras. In this paper we show the extent to which weights determine simple modules and use this to describe induction and restriction along isogenies. We also describe the behaviour of hermitian duals and unitarity under induction and restriction along isogenies.

</details>


### [121] [Base Change Fundamental Lemma for Central Elements in Depth-zero Hecke Algebras over Local Function Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10073)
*Weimin Jiang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在正特征局部函数域上，针对非分歧群引入了从扭曲共轭类到共轭类的抽象范数映射，通过稳定化（扭曲）迹公式，证明了正则半单元素对应的基变换基本引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征局部函数域（特征p>0）的非分歧群上建立抽象范数映射，并证明基变换基本引理，以完善Langlands纲领中关于迹公式稳定化的理论基础。

Method: 引入抽象范数映射联系扭曲共轭类与共轭类，利用稳定化（扭曲）迹公式的方法，针对正则半单元素进行证明。

Result: 成功证明了在正特征局部函数域上，非分歧群的正则半单元素满足基变换基本引理。

Conclusion: 该工作为局部函数域上的基变换理论提供了关键支撑，拓展了迹公式稳定化方法在正特征情形下的适用范围。

Abstract: Let $G$ be an unramified group over a local function field of characteristic $p>0$. This article introduces an abstract norm map between the twisted conjugacy classes and conjugacy classes of $G$ in the positive characteristics setting, applies the process of stabilization of (twisted) trace formula and proves the corresponding base change fundamental lemma for regular semisimple elements.

</details>


### [122] [Homological rigidity of quiver representations over $\mathbb{F}_1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10253)
*Changjian Fu,Liang Yang,Zhiyuan Zeng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在虚拟域F1上的箭图表示范畴中，所有高于2阶的Yoneda扩张群都消失，因此该范畴的整体维数被普遍限制为2，并根据箭图的方向结构对其同调维数进行了完全分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该范畴本质上是非可加的，无法应用经典同调代数工具，因此需要建立同调刚性现象来理解其同调性质。

Method: 通过分析虚拟域F1上箭图表示范畴的结构，证明了高阶Yoneda扩张群的消失性质。

Result: 所有高于2阶的Yoneda扩张群消失，整体维数不超过2；根据方向结构对箭图的同调维数进行了完全分类。

Conclusion: 该范畴的整体维数被普遍限制为2，且同调维数完全由箭图的方向结构决定。

Abstract: We establish a homological rigidity phenomenon for the category of representations of quivers over the virtual field $\mathbb{F}_1$, which is inherently non-additive and does not admit classical homological algebra tools. We prove that all higher Yoneda extension groups vanish beyond degree two for arbitrary quivers, including infinite ones. Consequently, the global dimension of the category is universally bounded by 2. Moreover, we obtain a complete classification of quivers according to their homological dimension, which is determined solely by the underlying orientation structure.

</details>


### [123] [Block-exoticity of reduction simple fusion systems on $S(n,p)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10264)
*Jun Liao,Yikun Liu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明对于$p\geq 13$且$5\leq n\leq p-3$，有限$p$-群$S(n,p)$上的每个约化简单融合系都是块-异域的，并建立了三个无限族约化简单奇异融合系的块-异域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限$p$-群上的融合系，特别是约化简单融合系的块-异域性，以深入理解群表示论和模表示论中的结构。

Method: 通过严格的数学证明，针对特定参数范围$p\geq 13$和$5\leq n\leq p-3$，分析有限$p$-群$S(n,p)$上的融合系性质，并推广到三个无限族。

Result: 在给定参数条件下，$S(n,p)$上的每个约化简单融合系都是块-异域的；同时三个无限族的约化简单奇异融合系也具有块-异域性。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了融合系理论中块-异域性的已知结果，为约化简单融合系的分类提供了新的约束条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that for $p\geq 13$ and $5\leq n\leq p-3$, every reduction simple fusion system on the finite $p$-group $S(n,p)$ is block-exotic. Furthermore, we establish the block-exoticity of three infinite families of reduction simple exotic fusion systems.

</details>


### [124] [Bump-Friedberg type periods beyond the cuspidal spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10289)
*Shenghao Li*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了GL₂ₙ上扭曲的Bump–Friedberg周期及其变体在尖点谱之外的连续延拓，并引入GL₂ₙ₊₁上的新周期，通过Eisenstein级数将该周期表示为L函数特殊值与局部zeta积分的有穷和，验证了Ben-Zvi–Sakellaridis–Venkatesh全局数值猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Bump–Friedberg型周期的研究从尖点谱扩展到更一般的自守函数空间，并验证Langlands纲领下的全局数值猜想。

Method: 通过Whittaker型zeta积分建立周期的连续延拓；利用Eisenstein级数展开将周期分解为L函数特殊值与局部zeta积分的有限和；结合全局Langlands对应与对偶群的不动点结构进行分析。

Result: 扭曲的Bump–Friedberg周期在适当正则性条件下可连续延拓至一致缓增的自守函数；GL₂ₙ₊₁上的新周期可表示为L函数特殊值与局部zeta积分的有穷和，且所得L因子与Ben-Zvi–Sakellaridis–Venkatesh猜想的切空间预测一致。

Conclusion: 该结果支撑了全局数值猜想的正确性，并为更一般约化群上周期积分的Langlands函子性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this article, we study several Bump--Friedberg type periods beyond the cuspidal spectrum. We first consider the twisted Bump--Friedberg period on $\textnormal{GL}_{2n}$, as well as a variant on $\textnormal{GL}_1\times \textnormal{GL}_{2n}$. Under suitable regularity conditions on the cuspidal datum, these periods extend continuously to automorphic functions of uniform moderate growth. Such extensions are characterized by entire Whittaker-type zeta integrals. We then introduce a Bump--Friedberg type period on $\textnormal{GL}_{2n+1}$, integrating over the subgroup $\textnormal{SL}_{n+1}\times \textnormal{GL}_n$. For certain Eisenstein series, we evaluate this period as a finite sum of products of special values of $L$-functions and normalized local zeta integrals. Assuming the expected global Langlands correspondence, the sum is indexed by the fixed points of the extended $L$-parameter on the conjectural dual variety, and the resulting $L$-factors agree with the tangent space prediction of the global numerical conjecture of Ben-Zvi-Sakellaridis-Venkatesh.

</details>


### [125] [Symmetry for the spin highest weight module $V(Λ_n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10327)
*Ola Amara-Omari*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究 $A^{(2)}_{2n}$ 型仿射李代数 $\mathfrak g$ 的最高权模 $V(Λ)$（其中 $Λ=Λ_n$），给出了一个特殊的分拆集，并得到了关于典范基元的若干结果。证明了在固定 $n$-content 下权重的对称性（该对称性围绕通过向空分拆添加零根的倍数得到的权重）。同时找到了一个重要典范基元集合中量子参数 $q$ 的最高幂次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入理解 $A^{(2)}_{2n}$ 型仿射李代数 $V(Λ_n)$ 模的结构，特别是其典范基元的分拆组合描述以及权空间的对称性质。

Method: 利用分拆理论（固定 $n$-content 的分拆集）来参数化典范基元；通过分析空分拆添加零根倍数得到的权重来揭示权重对称性；通过计算量子参数 $q$ 的最高幂次来研究典范基元的性质。

Result: 得到了 $V(Λ_n)$ 的一个特殊分拆集；证明了固定 $n$-content 下权重的对称性；找到了重要典范基元集合中 $q$ 的最高幂次。

Conclusion: 本文为 $A^{(2)}_{2n}$ 型仿射李代数的表示论提供了新的分拆组合解释，特别是对典范基元的结构和权重对称性有了更深入的认识。

Abstract: For an affine Lie algebra $\mathfrak g$ of type $A^{(2)}_{2n}$ we get a special set of partitions for the highest weight modules $V(Λ)$, where $Λ=Λ_n$, and we also get a some results for the canonical basis elements. We demonstrate a symmetry of weights with fixed $n $-content around the weights gotten from the empty partition by adding multiples of the null root. We also find the highest power of the quantum parameter $q$ in an important set of canonical basis elements.

</details>


### [126] [The Aubert and Bernstein involutions for disconnected groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10660)
*Yongshen Cheng,Tasho Kaletha*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将对Aubert定义的光滑有限长复表示范畴的对偶性以及Bernstein定义的上同调类比扩展到任意不连通约化p进群，证明了这些函子的唯一性、不可约性保持、与抛物诱导和限制的兼容性以及特征标公式等性质，并给出了不连通约化p进群上Steinberg表示的定义、特征标及其扭内视性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有对连通约化p进群表示论的深刻结果（Aubert对偶和Bernstein上同调对偶）推广至更一般的不连通情形，填补理论空白，并为研究不连通群表示提供系统工具。

Method: 通过函子构造与性质证明的方式，在任意不连通约化p进群上建立对偶函子，并系统验证其唯一性、不可约性保持、与抛物诱导/限制的兼容性以及特征标公式等关键性质。

Result: 成功将Aubert对偶和Bernstein上同调对偶推广到不连通约化p进群，并证明了该对偶函子的系列良好性质；作为应用，给出了Steinberg表示的定义、特征标公式及其扭内视性质讨论。

Conclusion: 该工作为不连通约化p进群的表示论提供了统一的对偶框架，为进一步研究不连通群的自守表示、内视理论以及L-函子性猜想等奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We extend to arbitrary disconnected reductive $p$-adic groups the duality on the category of smooth finite-length complex representations defined by Aubert, as well as its cohomological analog defined by Bernstein, and prove various properties of these functors, such as uniqueness, preservation of irreducibility, compatibility with parabolic induction and restriction, and a character formula. As a sample application, we obtain a definition of the Steinberg representation for a disconnected reductive $p$-adic group, compute its character, and discuss its twisted endoscopic properties.

</details>


### [127] [$τ$-Hochschild (co)homology, the square of the Serre bimodule, and the Coxeter automorphism of the Tamarkin--Tsygan calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10913)
*Marco Armenta*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 论文建立了有限维代数的τ-Hochschild（上）同调与Tamarkin-Tsygan微积分的Coxeter自同构σ_Λ之间的联系，证明了Nakayama函子将Happel极小分解转化为Serre双模的复形，τ-平移τ_nΛ恰好是该复形的循环双模，并给出了扩展序列。在整体维数有限时，τ_dΛ与Iyama-Oppermann的(d+1)-预投射代数的对偶相关；对于自入射代数，导出部分消失，解释了τ-上同调的增长。Euler示性数恢复Happel迹公式。证明了两种细化是横截的，提出了结合的Morita不变量，并给出了导出等价代数具有相同维数但相反合成的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维代数的Hochschild理论的两个近期推广——基于Iyama高Auslander-Reiten平移的τ-Hochschild（上）同调与Tamarkin-Tsygan微积分的Coxeter自同构σ_Λ——联系起来，揭示它们之间的结构关系，并建立统一的Morita不变量。

Method: 通过分析包络代数的Nakayama函子作用在Happel极小分解上得到的复形，证明该复形表示DΛ⊗_Λ DΛ（Serre双模的平方），其移位生成σ_Λ；证明τ-平移τ_nΛ恰好是该复形的循环双模；构造扩展序列并分析其项；利用Euler示性数恢复Happel迹公式；通过导出等价代数的例子展示τ-平移的行为。

Result: 建立了τ_nΛ与Serre双模复形循环双模的等同关系；得到扩展序列0→B_n→τ_nΛ→Tor_n^Λ(DΛ,DΛ)→0，其中外项对偶于Ext^n_{Λ^e}(Λ,Λ^e)，内项是极小模型的Morita理论剩余；在整体维数d时剩余消失，τ_dΛ对偶于Iyama-Oppermann的(d+1)-预投射代数的一次分量；对自入射代数导出部分恒为零；Euler示性数恢复Happel迹公式；两种细化是横截的；构造了导出等价但τ-平移具有相同维数相反合成的例子。

Conclusion: τ-Hochschild理论与Coxeter自同构通过Serre双模复形自然联系起来，这一联系统一了Hochschild理论的两种推广，提供了新的Morita不变量，并提出了导出等价下τ-Hochschild理论在光滑点上的不变性问题。

Abstract: We relate two recent enrichments of the Hochschild theory of a finite-dimensional algebra $\Lm$: the $τ$-Hochschild (co)homology of Cibils, Lanzilotta, Marcos and Solotar, built from Iyama's higher Auslander--Reiten translates of the regular bimodule, and the Coxeter automorphism $σ_\Lm$ of the Tamarkin--Tsygan calculus. We show that the Nakayama functor of the enveloping algebra transforms Happel's minimal resolution into a complex representing $\D\Lm\Ltimes_\Lm \D\Lm$, the square of the Serre bimodule whose shift generates $σ_\Lm$, and that the $τ$-translates $τ_n\Lm$ are precisely the cycle bimodules of this complex. This produces extensions $0\to \B_n\to τ_n\Lm\to \Tor_n^\Lm(\D\Lm,\D\Lm)\to 0$ whose outer term is dual to $\Ext^n_{\Lme}(\Lm,\Lme)$ and whose inner term is a strictly Morita-theoretic residue of the minimal model. In top degree $d=\gldim\Lm$ the residue vanishes and $τ_d\Lm$ is the dual of the degree-one component of the $(d+1)$-preprojective algebra of Iyama--Oppermann; for $\Lm=\kk Q$ hereditary, $τ_{\Lme}\Lm\cong \DΠ(Q)_1$ and $\HH^1_τ(\kk Q)$ is the degree-one part of the zeroth Hochschild homology of the preprojective algebra. For self-injective algebras, the derived part vanishes identically, which explains structurally the growth of $τ$-cohomology for the Buchweitz--Green--Madsen--Solberg algebras. Taking Euler characteristics in the Cibils--Lanzilotta--Marcos--Solotar dimension formulas recovers Happel's trace formula $\sum_i(-1)^i\dim\HH^i(\Lm)=-\trσ_\Lm$. We prove that the two refinements are transversal, propose the combined Morita invariant, exhibit derived-equivalent algebras of finite global dimension whose $τ$-translates have identical dimension but opposite composition, and pose the problem of derived invariance of $τ$-Hochschild theory over the smooth locus.

</details>


### [128] [$\mathfrak{so}_n(\mathbb{C})$-modules which are free over an abelian nilradical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11100)
*Yang Chen,Haijun Tan,Lu Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文分类了$\mathfrak{so}_n(\mathbb{C})$-模的一个范畴，该范畴的模在极大抛物子代数的交换幂零根基上的限制是秩为1的自由模。证明了这些模在一般情况下是单模，并在非单模情形给出了子模的显式描述。该范畴体现了权模与非权模之间的某种联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$\mathfrak{so}_n(\mathbb{C})$-模中一类特殊的模，其限制在极大抛物子代数的交换幂零根基上的自由性为1，旨在揭示权模与非权模之间的内在联系。

Method: 通过对$\mathfrak{so}_n(\mathbb{C})$-模进行分类，分析其在极大抛物子代数交换幂零根基上的限制性质，并研究模的单性及子模结构。

Result: 证明了这类模在一般情况下是单模，并给出了非单模情形下子模的显式描述。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个联系权模与非权模的范畴，深化了对李代数模结构的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we classify the category of $\mathfrak{so}_n(\mathbb{C})$-modules whose restrictions to the universal enveloping algebra of the abelian nilradical of a maximal parabolic subalgebra are free of rank $1$. We show that they generically are simple, and give an explicit description of submodules in the non-simple case. This category embodies a certain connection between weight modules and non-weight modules.

</details>


### [129] [FFLV bases for covariant representations of $\mathfrak{gl}(m|n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11133)
*Ibrahim Ahmad,Pranav Enugandla*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般线性李超代数 $\mathfrak{gl}(m|n)$ 上协变表示的 PBW 滤过，证明了对于形如 $(\lambda|\mu,0^{n-1})$ 的协变权，存在一个格点多面体，其格点参数化对应伴随分次空间的一组基。由此得到 $GL(m|n)$ 的部分旗超簇退化为环面超簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李超代数 $\mathfrak{gl}(m|n)$ 上协变表示的 PBW 滤过结构，并建立其与格点多面体及退化的环面超簇之间的联系。

Method: 针对 $\mathfrak{gl}(m|n)$ 的协变表示，考虑特定形式的协变权 $(\lambda|\mu,0^{n-1})$，利用 PBW 滤过构造伴随分次空间，并证明存在一个格点多面体使得其格点与该分次空间的基一一对应。

Result: 对于形如 $(\lambda|\mu,0^{n-1})$ 的协变权，证明了存在一个格点多面体，其格点参数化 PBW 滤过的伴随分次空间的一组基。进而得到了 $GL(m|n)$ 的部分旗超簇退化为环面超簇。

Conclusion: 建立了李超代数表示论中 PBW 滤过与格点多面体及环面退化之间的桥梁，为超簇的退化理论提供了新的结果。

Abstract: We study the PBW filtration on covariant representations for the Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{gl}(m|n)$. We prove for all covariant weights of the form $(λ|μ,0^{n-1})$, that there exists a lattice polytope such that the lattice points of this polytope parametrize a basis of the corresponding associated graded space. As a consequence, we obtain degenerations of partial flag supervarieties for the supergroup $GL(m|n)$ into toric supervarieties.

</details>


### [130] [Balanced notation for $τ$-rigid pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11456)
*Kiyoshi Igusa,Gordana Todorov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对有限维代数上的τ-刚性对提出了一种新的“平衡对”记法 A/B，简化了τ-倾斜理论中的许多公式，并使Jasso范畴、挠类、挠自由类以及符号例外序列等对偶概念更易比较。该记法在视觉上与半不变量的墙标签匹配，并证明了在函子有限情形下，Igusa-Maresca的挠类公式与Adachi-Iyama-Reiten的标准公式一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的τ-刚性对记法在表达对偶概念（如挠类与挠自由类、符号与余符号例外序列）时不够简洁直观，公式形式复杂。作者希望提出一种新的记法，统一并简化这些表达，同时增强视觉直观性。

Method: 提出“平衡对”记法 A/B 来表示τ-刚性对；在该记法下重新表述τ-倾斜理论中的关键概念和公式（如Jasso范畴、挠类、挠自由类、符号例外序列）；并与现有文献中的公式进行对比验证。

Result: 平衡记法显著简化了τ-倾斜理论中的公式表达；使对偶概念（如符号与余符号例外序列）更易于比较；在视觉上与遗传情形的半不变量墙标签匹配，在一般情形下也近似匹配；在函子有限情形下，Igusa-Maresca的挠类公式与Adachi-Iyama-Reiten的标准公式在平衡记法下等价。

Conclusion: 平衡记法 A/B 是表示τ-刚性对的一种自然且有效的工具，它简化了理论表达、增强了对偶性的可视化理解，并统一了不同文献中的公式。

Abstract: Over any finite dimensional algebra $Λ$ over any field $K$, we propose a new notation for $τ$-rigid pairs: We represent these as ``balanced pairs'' $\frac AB$, We show that many formulas in $τ$-tilting theory are simplified in the balanced notation. For example, the Jasso category and its dual, the torsion class and torsion-free class associated to any $τ$-tilting pair. Other dual concepts, such as signed and cosigned exceptional sequences are also easier to compare using the balanced notation. Balanced notation is also visually appealing because it matches the wall labels for the semi-invariant picture in the hereditary case and almost matches the wall labels in the general case. We also show that, in the functorially finite case, the formulas for torsion classes given by Igusa and Maresca [11] agree with the standard formulas of Adachi, Iyama and Reiten [1] when expressed in balanced notation.

</details>


### [131] [Global dimension of a string algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11552)
*Zheng Xin,Lingchun Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文利用组合方法刻画了弦代数的整体维数，并建立了弦代数整体维数为无穷的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦代数是一类重要的代数结构，其整体维数是衡量代数复杂性的关键同调不变量。此前缺乏利用组合方法系统刻画弦代数整体维数的研究。

Method: 采用组合方法，通过分析弦代数的箭图与关系结构，对整体维数进行组合刻画。

Result: 给出了弦代数整体维数的组合特征描述，并建立了整体维数为无穷的充要条件。

Conclusion: 通过组合方法成功刻画了弦代数的整体维数，为理解这类代数的同调性质提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we characterize the global dimension of a string algebra by using combinatorial methods. Moreover, we establish a necessary and sufficient condition for when the global dimension of a string algebra is infinite.

</details>


### [132] [Mackeyfication of equivariant categories I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11767)
*Paul Balmer,Hatice Mutlu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构建了"Mackey化"操作，将等变范畴（equvariant categories）左右逼近为Mackey 2-函子，受Boltje在普通Mackey 1-函子上的工作启发，并通过mark变换联系左右Mackey化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变范畴作为依赖有限群G的加性范畴族，其2-函子性存在不足——诱导（induction）结构缺失。为了解决这一缺陷，需要以最小方式扩充范畴使得诱导得以出现。

Method: 借鉴Boltje在Mackey 1-函子上的构造思路，分别从左右两侧对等变范畴进行"mackeyfication"（Mackey化）逼近，构造出Mackey 2-函子；同时引入mark变换建立左右Mackey化之间的联系。

Result: 成功构造了等变范畴的左Mackey化和右Mackey化，并建立了二者之间的mark变换关系，最后通过具体例子加以说明。

Conclusion: Mackey化提供了一种系统方法，使等变范畴成为真正的Mackey 2-函子，从而弥补了诱导缺失的问题，并建立了左右Mackey化之间的对偶联系。

Abstract: Colloquially speaking, `equivariant categories' refer to families of additive categories $\mathcal{A}(G)$ depending 2-functorially on a finite group $G$. We construct approximations of equivariant categories by Mackey 2-functors, both on the left and on the right. The idea is to enlarge $\mathcal{A}$ in a minimal way to make induction appear. These `mackeyfications' are inspired by Boltje's work with ordinary Mackey 1-functors. We also relate our left and right mackeyfications via a mark transformation. Finally we discuss examples.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用LHCb探测器收集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子碰撞数据，对B⁻→Λc⁺p̅π⁻衰变进行振幅分析，在Λc⁺π⁻不变质量谱中观测到已知的Σc(2455)⁰、Σc(2520)⁰、Σc(2800)⁰重子，以及两个新态Σc(2900)⁰和Σc(3200)⁰，并确定了三个高质量态的Breit-Wigner质量和宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展粲重子谱学图景，理解粲重子结构与动力学。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在质心能量7、8、13 TeV下收集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子碰撞数据，对B⁻→Λc⁺p̅π⁻衰变进行振幅分析。

Result: 观测到已知的Σc(2455)⁰、Σc(2520)⁰、Σc(2800)⁰重子，以及两个新重子态Σc(2900)⁰和Σc(3200)⁰，并确定了三个高质量态的Breit-Wigner质量和宽度。

Conclusion: 扩展了粲重子谱学图景，为理解粲重子结构与动力学提供了关键数据。

Abstract: An amplitude analysis of the $B^-\rightarrow \itΛ^+_c\overline{p}π^{-}$ decay is performed using proton-proton collision data, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $9\text{ fb}^{-1}$, collected with the \mbox{LHCb} detector at center-of-mass energies of 7, 8, and 13$\text{ TeV}$. In the $\itΛ^+_cπ^{-}$ invariant-mass spectrum, the known $\itΣ_c(2455)^0$, $\itΣ_c(2520)^0$, $\itΣ_c(2800)^0$ baryons are observed, along with two new states: $\itΣ_c(2900)^0$ and $\itΣ_c(3200)^0$. The masses and widths of the three high-mass states, modeled with Breit--Wigner line shapes, are determined. This result expands the landscape of charm baryon spectroscopy and provides crucial data for understanding charm baryon structure and dynamics.

</details>

Y. Zhao,H. Zheng,H. Zhong,M. Ziembicki,M. Zito*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了T2K ND280探测器首次测量氧靶上的缪子中微子带电流相互作用截面，以运动学不平衡量为函数，末态无π介子且至少含一个质子。该截面同时针对碳和氧靶提取，并双重微分于多个KI观测量，为核效应建模提供新见解。联合测量直接敏感于两种靶核间的关联，有助于降低T2K和Hyper-Kamiokande等中微子振荡实验的系统不确定性。与主流中微子事件生成器的对比显示，没有模型能完全描述所有相空间区域的数据，结果指出了当前及未来中微子振荡实验中中微子-原子核相互作用建模需要改进的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡实验依赖于精确的中微子-原子核相互作用模型，而核效应（如末态相互作用、核子关联等）是当前最大的系统误差来源之一。现有模型在描述不同靶核（碳和氧）上的相互作用时存在显著不确定性，尤其在运动学不平衡量这一可揭示核内动态过程的观测量上缺乏实验约束。

Method: 利用T2K ND280探测器，首次测量氧靶上缪子中微子带电流相互作用截面（末态无π介子、至少一个质子），以运动学不平衡量为函数，同时提取碳和氧靶的截面，并进行双重微分分析。

Result: 首次获得了氧靶上的KI微分截面数据，并与碳靶联合提取；与多个主流中微子事件生成器（如GENIE、NEUT等）的预测对比发现，现有模型均无法在所有相空间区域内完全描述实验数据。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了当前中微子-原子核相互作用模型在核效应描述上的不足，明确了模型改进的方向，对降低T2K和Hyper-Kamiokande等实验的系统不确定性具有重要意义。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of muon-neutrino charged-current cross section as a function of kinematic imbalance (KI) observables on oxygen with no pions and at least one proton in the final state, using the T2K ND280 detector. The cross section is extracted simultaneously for carbon and oxygen targets and double-differentially as a function of several KI observables, providing new insight into the modeling of nuclear effects. This joint measurement offers direct sensitivity to the correlations between two targets, a key ingredient for reducing systematic uncertainties in neutrino oscillation experiments that employ multiple target nuclei, such as T2K and Hyper-Kamiokande. Comparisons with predictions from widely used neutrino event generators show that none of the models fully describe the data across all regions of measured phase space. These results highlight possible directions where improvements in neutrino-nucleus interaction modeling are needed for current and future neutrino oscillation experiments.

</details>
