*Ronald Sielinski*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于排序稳定性和结构充分性的序贯收敛框架，用于确定生成式搜索引擎的AI可见性测量何时达到足够的数据收集量，以支持有意义的比较分析。该框架无需预设查询数量或相关性目标，而是根据观测到的引用分布结构自适应判断收敛，并在30个平台-主题组合上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界缺乏一种原理性的方法来确定AI可见性测量中何时收集了足够的数据。不同研究和平台之间的数据收集预算差异很大，而结论往往基于稳定性和精度未知的排名，导致无法判断观测到的差异是否足以支持决策。

Method: 提出一个序贯收敛框架，包含两个互补标准：(1) 排序稳定性——评估秩相关轨迹是否达到结构平台期；(2) 结构充分性——评估已有领域（置信区间排除零的领域）之间的引用份额离散度是否超过这些估计的不确定性。两者均基于观测到的引用分布规律（排序结构、不确定性轮廓、已观测与已建立领域的边界）推导而来，无需外部指定查询数量、相关性目标或置信区间宽度目标。

Result: 在Gemini、SearchGPT和Perplexity三个平台上的30个平台-主题组合上应用该框架，结果表明没有固定的数据收集预算能够在不同上下文中被证明是合理的，收敛可以从观测分布的结构中评估。该框架能够适应平台和主题特定的引用分布特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为判断AI可见性测量何时足以支持比较分析提供了实用基础，解决了行业中长期缺乏的数据收集充分性判定问题。

Abstract: AI visibility measurement is comparative: practitioners want to know which domains generative search engines cite most often and whether observed differences are large enough to support decisions. Yet the industry lacks a principled way to determine whether enough data has been collected. Collection budgets vary widely across studies and platforms, and conclusions are often drawn from rankings whose stability and precision are unknown. We introduce a sequential convergence framework based on two complementary criteria: rank stability evaluates whether the rank-correlation trajectory has reached a structural plateau, while structural sufficiency evaluates whether the spread of citation shares among established domains -- those whose confidence intervals exclude zero -- exceeds the uncertainty of those estimates. Together, these criteria distinguish rankings that have merely stabilized from those sufficiently resolved to support inference. Both are derived from regularities in the observed citation distribution, including its rank structure, uncertainty profile, and the boundary between observed and established domains. The framework retains a small number of structural constants but requires no externally specified query count, correlation target, or confidence-interval width target; stopping is driven by observed measurement uncertainty and remains robust across a range of sufficiency thresholds. Applied across 30 platform-topic combinations spanning Gemini, SearchGPT, and Perplexity, the framework adapts to platform- and topic-specific citation distributions. Results show that no fixed collection budget can be justified across contexts and that convergence can instead be evaluated from the structure of the observed distribution. The framework provides a practical basis for determining when AI visibility measurements are ready to support comparative analysis.

</details>


### [243] [Age-specific demographic modeling and forecasting: Rolling window, expanding window, or both?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10527)
*Sizhe Chen,Han Lin Shang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合滚动窗口和扩展窗口的简单组合预测方法（等权重平均），用于年龄别死亡率和生育率的建模与预测。基于多国比较，发现某种窗口方法在不同预测区间上表现一致优越，而组合方法在没有先验信息时提供了稳健实用的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滚动窗口和扩展窗口在年龄别人口建模与预测中广泛使用，但两种方法的相对预测准确性缺乏系统比较，且在实际应用中往往缺乏选择某种窗口的先验信息，因此需要一种稳健的替代方案。

Method: 提出将滚动窗口与扩展窗口的预测结果进行等权重平均的组合方法。基于多国年龄别死亡率和生育率数据，对两种窗口类型分别建模并比较其预测精度，同时评估组合方法的性能。

Result: 多国比较显示，某种窗口方法（滚动或扩展）在不同预测区间上的一致性优越表现；组合方法在缺乏先验信息时提供了稳健的预测性能。

Conclusion: 在没有先验信息指导窗口选择的情况下，滚动与扩展窗口的等权重组合是一个稳健且实用的预测方案。

Abstract: Rolling and expanding windows are widely used in age-specific demographic modeling and forecasting. Building on these approaches, we propose a simple combination method that assigns equal weight to the forecasts from both schemes. Our focus is on evaluating and comparing the forecast accuracy of the two window types in modeling age-specific mortality and fertility. Based on the multi-country comparison, the superior performance of one method often persists across different forecast horizons. In the absence of prior information, our combined approach offers a robust and practical alternative.

</details>


### [244] [Inferring Inventory Dynamics from Supply Chain Networks: A Graph Learning Approach with Autonomous Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10642)
*Tiancheng Gao,Xiang Zhang,Wei Lan,Bin Liu*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 针对中小企业库存数据稀缺导致供需错配难以直接测量的问题，本文提出一个多代理半监督推理框架，通过构建生产函数约束的图机器学习模型推断企业库存变化，并利用计量经济验证代理和专家评审代理进行多维度验证，实现了无需真实标签即可有效验证预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业缺乏系统性的库存记录，导致标注训练数据极度稀缺，使得传统监督学习方法无法可靠验证企业层面的供需错配预测，亟需一种能够在无标签数据条件下进行有效推理和验证的方法。

Method: 1) 构建生产函数约束的图机器学习模型，从供应链网络拓扑中推断企业级库存变化；2) 计量经济验证代理并行加载五个计量模型（空间溢出、动态持续性、因果方向、冲击传导、供需预测），从互补维度生成结构化经济证据；3) 专家评审代理合成多代理证据，通过解决跨代理不一致性生成统一的可靠性评估。

Result: 在库存变化预测任务上展现出稳定的预测性能；多代理计量验证确认预测的库存动态在因果结构和网络传导机制上与已有经济理论高度一致；即使在缺乏真实观测值的情况下，该框架也能实现有效的预测验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的多代理框架成功将标签稀缺问题转化为结构化的协作任务，为中小企业在无标签数据条件下进行供需错配测量和预测验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Supply-demand mismatch represents a fundamental challenge in supply chain management, yet its direct measurement remains particularly elusive for small and medium-sized enterprises (SMEs).These firms typically lack systematic inventory records, leaving labeled training data critically scarce. Conventional supervised learning methods rely heavily on labeled samples, rendering them ill-equipped to reliably validate firm-level predictions under such data-scarce conditions. To resolve this unlabeled-data dilemma, we develop a multi-agent semi-supervised inference framework that reframes the label-scarcity problem as a structured, collaborative task distributed across specialized agents. We first construct a production-function-constrained graph machine learning model that infers firm-level inventory changes directly from supply chain network topology. A dedicated econometric validation agent then concurrently loads five econometric models (spanning spatial spillovers, dynamic persistence, causal direction, shock transmission, and supply-demand forecasting) to generates structured economic evidence from complementary dimensions. An expert review agent synthesizes the structured econometric evidence and produces a unified consistency assessment by resolving cross-agent inconsistencies. Empirical results demonstrate stable predictive performance on inventory-change forecasting tasks. Multi-agent econometric validation further confirms that predicted inventory dynamics align closely with established economic theory in terms of causal structure and network transmission mechanisms. Critically, the proposed agent framework enables effective prediction verification even when ground-truth observations are unavailable.

</details>


### [245] [LLM-Enhanced Dynamic Financial Knowledge Graphs for Cross-Entity Signal Propagation and alpha discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10932)
*Lin Zhang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的财务信息传播框架，通过构建动态知识图谱和社区感知传播机制，生成网络化金融信号（CIS和PIS），在模拟实验中展现出优于传统情感分析和直接LLM事件信号的投资预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融NLP主要衡量单一公司的文档级情感，忽略了财务信息通过供应链、客户、竞争对手和技术生态等网络进行跨实体传播的效应。

Method: 1) 利用LLM将非结构化金融文档转化为结构化经济状态变化事件；2) 提取显式和隐式企业关系构建动态金融知识图谱；3) 采用社区感知机制传播事件信号（社区内传播强于跨社区）；4) 提出CIS和PIS两种网络金融信号及相应计量检验方法。

Result: 1) 框架可准确恢复潜在网络结构并检测新投资生态系统的出现；2) 传播信号在预测能力上超越情感和直接LLM事件信号；3) 社区感知传播在排名信息系数和多空夏普比率上均优于五个嵌套基准；4) Russell 1000校准模拟验证了结果在稀疏网络、异质新闻覆盖、大市值波动和较小效应量下的稳健性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的社区感知信息传播框架能有效捕获跨实体财务信息扩散，产生具有增量预测能力的网络化金融信号，对实证资产定价和投资组合构建具有重要价值。

Abstract: Financial information rarely affects a single company in isolation. Earnings surprises, capital expenditure changes, supply constraints, and guidance revisions can propagate through networks of suppliers, customers, competitors, and technology ecosystems. Traditional financial NLP primarily measures document-level sentiment for the directly mentioned company and often ignores cross-entity information diffusion.
  This paper develops an LLM-based financial measurement and signal propagation framework. The LLM converts unstructured financial documents into structured economic state-change events and extracts explicit and implicit corporate relationships to construct a dynamic financial knowledge graph. Event signals are then propagated through the estimated network using a community-aware mechanism, allowing information to diffuse more strongly within dynamically detected economic communities than across community boundaries.
  We introduce Community Information Surprise, CIS, and Propagated Information Surprise, PIS, as network-based financial signals and develop corresponding econometric tests. Controlled simulations with time-varying economic communities show that the framework accurately recovers latent network structure, detects the emergence of new investment ecosystems, and generates propagated signals with incremental predictive power beyond sentiment and direct LLM event signals. Across repeated simulations, community-aware propagation achieves the strongest rank information coefficient and long-short Sharpe ratio among five nested benchmarks.A second Russell 1000 calibrated simulation confirms that the main results persist under sparser networks, heterogeneous news coverage, realistic large-cap volatility, and smaller effect sizes.

</details>


### [246] [Climate-Invariant Conformal Prediction Intervals for Multi-Horizon Solar and Wind Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11470)
*Shreedhar Gangwar,Abhinav Bains,Banalaxmi Brahma*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于bootstrap多样化的XGBoost集成的异方差、非对称、组条件分割共形框架，用于太阳能和风能发电的不确定性量化。该框架无需逐站点或逐时段调参，在四个气候迥异的站点上，对太阳辐照度和风速的1-12小时预测均能达到接近名义覆盖率的预测区间，并将区间分数相较于竞争基线降低高达35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测方法要么缺乏有限样本有效性，要么需要逐站点重新校准，单一模型难以在不同气候条件下迁移。电力系统运营商需要可靠的不确定性量化来权衡风险，而非仅依赖点预测。

Method: 提出异方差、非对称、组条件分割共形预测框架，采用bootstrap多样化的XGBoost集成，使预测区间宽度自适应地适应局部预测难度，同时保持无分布覆盖保证。

Result: 在四个横跨南北半球、气候不同的站点上，对太阳辐照度和风速的1-12小时预测，该框架达到接近名义覆盖率的预测区间，并将区间分数相较于竞争基线降低高达35%。校准度和锐度是方法本身的属性，而非站点特定调参的结果。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在不进行逐站点或逐时段调参的情况下，提供具有分布自由覆盖保证的自适应预测区间，显著优于现有基线方法，具有良好的跨气候区泛化能力。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty quantification is essential for integrating solar and wind generation into modern power systems, where operators must weigh risk rather than act on point forecasts alone. Existing probabilistic methods, however, often either lack finite-sample validity or require per-site recalibration, so a single model rarely transfers across the diverse climates of a dispersed generation fleet. This paper proposes a heteroscedastic, asymmetric, group-conditional split-conformal framework built on a bootstrap-diverse XGBoost ensemble, producing prediction intervals that adapt in width to local difficulty while retaining distribution-free coverage guarantees. A single fixed specification, with no per-site or per-horizon tuning, is evaluated across four climatologically distinct sites spanning both hemispheres, at horizons of 1 to 12 hours, for both solar irradiance and wind speed. The framework holds near-nominal coverage on both targets and reduces the Interval Score by up to 35% relative to competitive baselines, with the calibration and sharpness of its intervals shown to be properties of the method rather than of site-specific tuning.

</details>


### [247] [Multiple Instrument Methods Comparison by Precision weighted Deming Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11776)
*Douglas M Hawkins,Jessica J Kraker*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出将加权Deming回归从两台仪器比较扩展到多台仪器比较，开发了包括拟合、统计推断、残差分析和异常值检测诊断的完整算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床化学中仪器比较研究（MC研究）常需比较两台以上仪器，但现有的Deming回归方法仅支持两台仪器的比较，而多仪器场景下的精确加权建模方法尚缺乏。

Method: 将已有的两仪器加权Deming回归模型扩展至多仪器场景，开发了拟合算法、形式化推断方法、残差分析以及异常值检测与诊断算法。

Result: 实现了多仪器环境下考虑测量变异非恒定性的加权Deming回归的完整方法论框架。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多仪器比较中参数化误差变量回归方法的空白，为临床化学等领域的仪器对比研究提供了更全面的统计工具。

Abstract: In methods comparison (MC) studies, specimens are tested using two or more instruments with the objective of establishing the statistical relationship between the different instruments readings. Unlike regular regression, this is an errors in variables problem. Relationships may be fitted parametrically (Deming regression) or non-parametrically (Passing Bablok or PB regression.) In clinical chemistry settings, the measurement variability is rarely constant, but generally increases with increasing analyte values. Precision weighted Deming regression models this variability and incorporates it into the fitting. The simplest setting of comparing two instruments is discussed in (1) and implemented in an R package (2). PB makes minimal distributional assumptions. Its classical two-instrument implementation has recently been extended to multiple instruments (3). This work extends the two-instrument Deming model of (1) to multiple instruments, developing algorithms for fitting, for formal inference, for residual analysis, and for outlier detection and diagnosis.

</details>


### [248] [Improved Global Ocean Heat Content Estimation by Modeling Vertical Spatio-Temporal Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11832)
*Thea Sukianto,Donata Giglio,Mikael Kuusela*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双变量局部平稳高斯过程和条件模拟的联合映射方法，用于估算2004-2022年Argo浮标数据中的海洋热含量（OHC），并通过建模两层压力层之间的相关性，将全球OHC异常的不确定性降低了最多15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确估算海洋热含量及其不确定性对于理解地球能量失衡和气候演变至关重要。以往研究将海洋分为至少两个压力层分别映射，导致求和时总OHC的不确定性估算复杂化。本文旨在通过联合建模两层之间的相关性来改进映射和不确定性量化方法。

Method: 使用2004-2022年Argo剖面浮标数据，采用双变量局部平稳高斯过程和条件模拟方法，对两个压力层（浅层和深层）进行联合映射，同时建模两层之间的相关性，而不是分别独立映射。

Result: 建模两层相关性后，全球OHC异常映射得到改善，不确定性相比独立映射方法最多降低15%。该方法能够更可靠地评估区域和全球尺度上OHC异常的统计显著性。

Conclusion: 联合映射方法通过考虑两层压力层之间的相关性，有效降低了OHC估算的不确定性，为理解气候系统中的能量分布提供了更可靠的统计工具。

Abstract: Estimating ocean heat content (OHC) with reliable uncertainties is critical for understanding and monitoring the evolution of Earth's climate, as the ocean has stored most of the energy accumulated in the climate system due to Earth Energy Imbalance. Here, we use Argo profiling float data from 2004-2022 to map OHC. As fewer Argo observations are available deeper in the water column, previous studies have partitioned the ocean into at least two pressure layers and mapped each separately, which complicates the estimation of uncertainties when the maps are summed to get the total OHC. In this work, we consider the case of two pressure layers and propose an improved mapping and uncertainty quantification method using bivariate locally stationary Gaussian processes and conditional simulations to map the two sections jointly while accounting for the correlation between them. We find that modeling this correlation results in improved OHC anomaly mapping and up to a 15 percent reduction of global OHC anomaly uncertainties in comparison to mapping the two layers separately without accounting for their dependence. These estimated uncertainties are essential to analyze the statistical significance of OHC anomalies on both regional and global scales, which we demonstrate using several climatological case studies.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Error analysis for 3D Navier--Stokes equations with additive noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09926)
*Soumya Ranjan Behera,Dominic Breit,Abhishek Chaudhary*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维随机Navier-Stokes方程的半隐式欧拉和Crank-Nicolson时间离散格式，证明了局部时间上半隐式欧拉格式收敛阶为1，Crank-Nicolson格式收敛阶为3/2，并通过数值模拟验证了理论结果。这是首次对三维随机Navier-Stokes方程进行数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维随机Navier-Stokes方程的时间离散化收敛性，填补该领域数值模拟的理论空白。

Method: 采用半隐式欧拉格式和Crank-Nicolson格式进行时间离散化，结合加性随机强迫，进行理论收敛性分析并辅以大规模数值模拟验证。

Result: 半隐式欧拉格式局部收敛阶为1，Crank-Nicolson格式局部收敛阶为3/2，数值模拟结果与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 两种格式在三维随机Navier-Stokes方程中具有明确的收敛速率，且首次成功实现了该方程的数值模拟。

Abstract: We consider the three-dimensional stochastic Navier--Stokes equations with additive stochastic forcing. We study temporal discretizations based on a semi-implicit Euler as well as a Crank-Nicolson scheme. We prove that locally in time the former converges with rate 1 while the latter converges with rate 3/2. These theoretical results are confirmed by extensive numerical simulations. To the best of our knowledge this is the first time that numerical simulations for the three-dimensional stochastic Navier--Stokes equations have been performed.

</details>


### [250] [Optimal history encoding for elastic-plastic hereditary laws: Sharp input and constitutive approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09974)
*Michael Ortiz,Pablo Pedregal*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了率无关弹-理想塑性材料在遗传形式下的本构关系，并探讨了通过有限历史替代模型进行近似的方法。在材料点层面，本构律是由度量投影到应力空间闭凸弹性域生成的向量play算子。对于绝对连续驱动历史，本构律具有微分形式。通过将历史近似为最多N个常值段的右连续阶梯替代，证明了输入近似的极小极大定理，并提出了材料律感知的编码器以实现最优本构近似，其最优速率为(2N)^{-1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将率无关弹-理想塑性响应直接表述为遗传形式，并研究通过有限历史替代模型进行近似的理论基础，为计算塑性力学中历史依赖型本构模型的降阶和压缩提供严格的数学框架。

Method: 采用向量play算子描述本构律，建立W^{1,1}框架下的遗传律性质（因果性、变差收缩、BV-to-L^∞稳定性）；通过等变差采样实现输入的L^∞最优近似；引入应力感知编码器，允许压缩精确应力历史而非仅驱动历史，实现本构近似的最优极小极大定理。

Result: 证明了输入近似的极小极大定理：最优编码器由等变差采样给出。对于本构近似，应力感知编码器结合离散遗传译码器在非退化假设0∈int C下达到最优值(2N)^{-1}。标量互补变量情形下输入等变差编码器即为最优。

Conclusion: 累积变分是采样和压缩驱动历史与本构历史的自然变量，为率无关塑性本构模型的降阶近似提供了严格的误差界和最优策略。

Abstract: We formulate rate-independent elastic-ideally-plastic response directly in hereditary form and study its approximation by finite history surrogates. At the material-point level, the constitutive law is the vector play operator generated by metric projection onto a closed convex elastic domain in stress space. Starting from the closest-point return mapping for step inputs, we pass to absolutely continuous driving histories, for which the constitutive law admits a differential form: the stress remains in the elastic domain and the difference of rates belongs almost everywhere to the normal cone. In this $W^{1,1}$ setting, the hereditary law is causal, contracts variation, and satisfies a $BV$-to-$L^\infty$ stability estimate. We then approximate histories by right-continuous step surrogates with at most $N$ constant pieces. For absolutely continuous inputs, we prove a sharp minimax theorem for input approximation in $L^\infty$, normalized by the $BV$ norm: the optimal encoder is given by equal-variation sampling. For constitutive approximation, the correct vector-valued minimax statement is obtained by allowing the encoder to be material-law aware: it may compress the exact stress history $\mathcal P(π)$ rather than only the driving history $π$. The resulting stress-aware encoder, followed by the same discrete hereditary decoder, gives the sharp value $(2N)^{-1}$ under the natural nondegeneracy assumption $0\in\operatorname{int}C$. The scalar complementary-variable case is also recorded: there the input equal-variation encoder is sharp because the scalar stop/play operator is $L^\infty$-nonexpansive in the complementary variable. The results identify cumulative variation as the natural variable for sampling and compressing both driving and constitutive histories.

</details>


### [251] [Nonlinear approximation with adaptive dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10052)
*V. Temlyakov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于自适应字典的非线性逼近问题，该问题与函数类在最优线性采样恢复中的误差密切相关。不同于传统针对单个光滑函数类的研究，本文关注由一类积分算子（核函数来自给定函数类）所定义的函数类集合的采样恢复误差渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Kolmogorov宽度研究依赖经典双线性字典下的核稀疏逼近，而最优线性采样恢复误差的研究则需要考虑由核本身决定的自适应字典。本文旨在研究这一重要的自适应字典下的非线性逼近问题，并拓展一种针对函数类集合（而非单个光滑类）的采样恢复误差渐近行为分析的一般方法。

Method: 本文采用非线性逼近方法，研究自适应字典下的稀疏逼近问题；同时采用一种一般性框架，分析由核函数来自给定函数类的积分算子所定义的函数类集合的采样恢复误差渐近行为。

Result: 得到了自适应字典下非线性逼近问题的相关结果；给出了针对函数类集合（而非单个光滑类）的采样恢复误差渐近行为分析结果。

Conclusion: 本文发展了自适应字典下非线性逼近的理论，并将采样恢复误差分析从单个光滑函数类拓展到由一类积分算子定义的函数类集合，补充了先前在Kolmogorov宽度和熵数方面的研究。

Abstract: It is well known that the study of the Kolmogorov widths of a function class, which is the image of the unit ball of the $L_q$ space of an integral operator $J_K$ with the kernel $K$, is closely connected with the study of sparse approximations of the kernel $K$ with respect to the classical bilinear dictionary. Recently, it was discovered that if instead of the Kolmogorov widths we study the errors of optimal linear sampling recovery of the same classes, then we need to study sparse approximations of the kernel $K$ with respect to an adaptive dictionary, which is determined by the kernel $K$. In this paper we study this important problem of nonlinear approximation with respect to an adaptive dictionary. Also, in this paper we continue to develop the following general approach, which is related to the above nonlinear approximation problem. We study asymptotic behavior of the errors of sampling recovery not for an individual smoothness class, how it is usually done, but for the collection of classes, which are defined by integral operators with kernels coming from a given class of functions. Earlier, such approach was realized for the Kolmogorov widths and very recently for the entropy numbers.

</details>


### [252] [An asymptotic-preserving reduced-order method for parametrised rarefied gas flow by proper generalised decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10085)
*Luowei Yin,Wei Su*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于本征正交分解（PGD）提出了一种先验的、渐进保持的降阶方法，用于求解高维参数化Shakhov动理学模型方程，通过将原问题分解为少量低维问题来缓解维度灾难，并利用合成方程的解捕获流体力学渐近性，使求解器能自动退化为Navier-Stokes方程的宏观求解器，从而在保持一定精度的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻尔兹曼方程求解面临高维度和多特征尺度共存导致的巨大计算成本，难以满足工程设计仿真中的快速响应需求。

Method: 基于本征正交分解（PGD）的先验渐进保持降阶方法：通过构造低秩解的分离表示将高维问题分解为少量低维问题；将合成方程的解融入PGD算法以捕获流体力学渐近性；将稀化参数作为额外坐标，实现全参数空间的一次求解多次查询。

Result: 数值算例表明该方法能够以一定精度模拟稀薄气体流动，并显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提PGD降阶方法有效缓解了维度灾难，通过渐进保持特性自动衔接动理学区与连续流区，且参数化求解方式支持工程中的快速多查询需求。

Abstract: Modelling rarefied gas flow using the Boltzmann equation is vital in many areas. Due to the high dimensionality and coexistence of multiple characteristic scales, conventional solution strategies to this equation incur prohibitively high computational costs and are inadequate for rapid response in engineering design simulations. Based on proper generalised decomposition (PGD), we propose an \textit{a priori}, asymptotic-preserving reduced-order method to solve the high-dimensional, parametrised Shakhov kinetic model equation. The method reduces the original problem to a few low-dimensional problems by formulating separated representations for the low-rank solution, thereby mitigating the curse of dimensionality. To capture the hydrodynamic asymptotics, we incorporated solutions of some synthetic equations into the PGD algorithm. This treatment allows the PGD solver to automatically reduce to a macroscopic solver for the Navier-Stokes equations, whose solution naturally exhibits low-rank structure. By treating the rarefaction parameter as an additional coordinate, a parametrised solution can be computed once and for all over the entire range of rarefaction, enabling fast multiple queries to any points in the parameter space. Numerical examples are presented to demonstrate the capability of the method to simulate rarefied gas flow with certain accuracy and a significant reduction in computational costs.

</details>


### [253] [Regularity and high-order time stepping for semilinear subdiffusion equations with singular initial data beyond the $L^\infty$ framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10100)
*Runjie Zhang,Dongling Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有奇异初值的半线性次扩散问题，在分数阶Sobolev空间中建立了数值格式的适定性与误差分析，通过利用解算子的平滑性质克服非线性项的奇异放大效应，并证明了指数卷积求积方法的时间点态误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统L∞框架无法处理奇异初值导致的解无界问题，且非线性项在弱空间中奇异性被放大，需要发展新的理论框架来分析该类问题的数值格式。

Method: 利用次扩散解算子的平滑性质，在分数阶算子空间中建立合适的非线性假设，将奇异性从非线性项转移到解算子中进行控制，进而分析指数卷积求积方法。

Result: 建立了温和解的适定性与正则性结果，推导出指数卷积求积方法的点态时间误差估计，数值实验验证了收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提出的分析框架有效克服了L∞框架的局限，为奇异初值次扩散问题的数值分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper aims to analyze a numerical scheme for semilinear subdiffusion problems with singular initial data beyond the $L^\infty$ framework. The main difficulty lies in the stronger singular behavior of the nonlinear term compared with previous analyses. Since the singular initial datum is too rough to guarantee a uniform $L^\infty$ bound for the solution, the usual Lipschitz framework in the base space is no longer sufficient. The analysis must instead be carried out in weaker fractional Sobolev-type spaces, where nonlinear composition is more delicate and the term $f(u(t))$ may exhibit an amplified singularity relative to that of $u(t)$. To overcome this difficulty, we exploit the smoothing properties of the subdiffusion solution operators and formulate suitable nonlinear assumptions in fractional operator spaces. These smoothing estimates allow part of the singularity to be transferred from the nonlinear term to the solution operators, where it can be controlled. Under these assumptions, we establish well-posedness and regularity results for the mild solution and derive a pointwise-in-time error estimate for the exponential convolution quadrature method. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence rates.

</details>


### [254] [On exponential convergence of Chebyshev polynomial approximation for multivariate analytic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10209)
*Xinyu Wang,Haiyong Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文利用多复变势理论，证明了在任意下闭凸多项式空间中，Chebyshev投影能达到与最佳多项式逼近相同的指数收敛速度，并将该分析推广到张量积Chebyshev插值、Gauss-Legendre求积、Padua插值与求积以及Chebyshev-Galerkin方法，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化多元解析函数的Chebyshev投影的指数收敛速度，并证明其与最佳多项式逼近具有相同的收敛速率。

Method: 基于多复变势理论（pluripotential theory）对Chebyshev投影进行新分析，并将其推广到张量积Chebyshev插值、张量积Gauss-Legendre求积、Padua插值与求积、Chebyshev-Galerkin方法等多种相关场景。

Result: 在下闭凸多项式空间中，Chebyshev投影的指数收敛速度与最佳多项式逼近相同；各推广方法（张量积插值、求积、Padua方法、Galerkin方法）均建立了相应的指数收敛速率。

Conclusion: Chebyshev投影在多元解析函数逼近中具有最优的指数收敛性能，且该分析框架可系统推广至多种数值方法，数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a new analysis of the Chebyshev projection for multivariate analytic functions, drawing on pluripotential theory. It is proved that in any downward closed convex polynomial space, the Chebyshev projection achieves the same exponential convergence rate as the best polynomial approximation. This result enables a precise quantification of the exponential convergence rate of the Chebyshev projection. The analysis is then extended to several related topics, including tensorized Chebyshev interpolation, tensor product Gauss--Legendre quadrature, Padua interpolation and cubature, and Chebyshev-Galerkin method, with the corresponding exponential convergence rate established in each case. Supporting numerical experiments are provided to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [255] [PnP-IPA: A Provably Convergent Plug-and-Play Inexact Proximal Algorithm for Nonconvex Imaging Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10223)
*Cristiano Parenti,Silvia Bonettini,Marco Prato*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出PnP-IPA（即插即用非精确近端算法）解决现有PnP方法在理论收敛保证上的局限性，通过新的分裂策略、自适应步长选择和Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质分析，实现非凸目标下的全局收敛，并在图像去模糊任务中取得最优恢复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PnP方法虽有经验成功，但缺乏严格收敛保证；基于梯度步（GS）去噪器的可证明方法存在正则化参数限制严格、步长规则僵化、无法处理非凸数据保真项等理论与实践瓶颈。

Method: 提出新的分裂策略，非精确地计算缩放隐式正则化器的近端算子；设计新型替代势函数驱动Armijo类回溯线搜索实现自适应步长选择；利用Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质建立非凸目标下到稳定点的全局收敛性，不施加正则化参数假设。

Result: 在高斯和柯西噪声下的图像去模糊实验中，PnP-IPA有效解除先前理论约束，实现最优参数调优，获得最先进的恢复质量和鲁棒收敛性能。

Conclusion: PnP-IPA克服了GS去噪器方法的理论和实际限制，在非凸场景下兼具严格收敛保证和优异实践效果。

Abstract: Plug-and-Play (PnP) methods have emerged as a highly effective paradigm for solving imaging inverse problems by replacing traditional proximity operators of regularization terms with highly expressive deep denoisers. While empirically successful, establishing rigorous convergence guarantees for PnP algorithms remains a major challenge. Existing provable approaches based on the Gradient-Step (GS) denoiser suffer from theoretical and practical limitations, such as restrictive bounds on the regularization parameter, rigid step-size rules, and the inability to handle nonconvex data-fidelity terms. In this paper, we introduce PnP-IPA (Plug-and-Play Inexact Proximal Algorithm), a novel optimization scheme that overcomes these bottlenecks. We propose a new splitting strategy that evaluates the proximal operator of the scaled implicit regularizer inexactly. To enable adaptive step-size selection without exact objective evaluations, we design a novel surrogate merit function that successfully drives an Armijo-like backtracking line-search. Relying on the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz property, we establish global convergence to a stationary point of the nonconvex objective without imposing any assumption on the regularization parameter. Extensive numerical experiments on image deblurring under both Gaussian and Cauchy noise demonstrate the practical advantages of PnP-IPA. By effectively lifting previous theoretical constraints, our method allows for optimal parameter tuning, yielding state-of-the-art restoration quality and robust convergence even in nonconvex regimes.

</details>


### [256] [Regarding the numerical diffusion of the HLLD-type scheme in "On energy consistency of intermediate states in HLL-type MHD Riemann solvers"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10293)
*Fan Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 对HLLD-ec格式进行简单修改，以消除Dai-Woodward激波管问题中因较强纵向磁场出现的密度振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始HLLD-ec格式（JCP, 2026）在处理强纵向磁场的Dai-Woodward激波管问题时存在未知的密度振荡缺陷，需要修正。

Method: 对HLLD-ec格式进行简单修改，具体修改细节需参考原文。

Result: 消除了Dai-Woodward激波管问题中因较强纵向磁场产生的密度振荡。

Conclusion: 所提出的简单修改能有效解决原始HLLD-ec格式在强纵向磁场下的密度振荡问题。

Abstract: A simple modification to the HLLD-ec scheme is introduced to remove a density oscillation that appears in the Dai-Woodward shock tube problem with a stronger longitudinal magnetic field. The original HLLD-ec scheme was introduced in "On energy consistency of intermediate states in HLL-type MHD Riemann solvers, Journal of Computational Physics, 553 (2026), 114724", and the authors were not aware of this issue.

</details>


### [257] [Direct Methods for Calculating PseudoInverses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10302)
*Jeff Knisley*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文重新审视并改进了直接计算Moore-Penrose伪逆的方法，使其克服了传统直接方法在秩揭示和条件数方面的局限性，提出了适用于大规模和稀疏多线性回归应用的新变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接计算伪逆的方法存在秩揭示能力差、条件数不佳或实际应用受限等问题，而基于SVD的方法计算成本高。本文旨在改进直接方法以克服这些局限性，使其适用于大规模或稀疏多线性回归场景。

Method: 重新解释了几种现有直接计算伪逆的方法，并在此基础上引入新的变体，特别针对大规模和稀疏多线性回归应用进行了优化设计。

Result: 改进后的直接方法克服了传统直接方法在秩揭示和条件数方面的局限性，能够有效应用于大规模或稀疏多线性回归问题。

Conclusion: 直接方法在克服传统局限性后，可以成为基于SVD的伪逆计算方法的有效替代方案，特别适合大规模和稀疏多线性回归应用。

Abstract: The Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse of a matrix can be defined and calculated using its singular value decomposition. There are also direct methods for computing matrix pseudo-inverses (those that avoid eigenvalue computations), but these are often rank-revealing, poorly conditioned, or otherwise limited in practice. In this paper, we demonstrate that direct methods can overcome these limitations. In particular, we reinterpret several existing direct methods and introduce new variations that are appropriate for large scale or sparse multilinear regression applications.

</details>


### [258] [Sketch-and-Restart: Randomized Sketching in Quadrature-Based Restarting for Matrix Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10354)
*Stefan Güttel,Jingyu Liu,Lauri Nyman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于计算大型稀疏非厄米矩阵函数作用于向量的 sketch-and-restart 框架，结合了基于求积的重启与 sketched/truncated Arnoldi 过程，并开发了两类重启算法（固定维度与自适应维度），实验证明了其在计算效率、存储和收敛速度上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型稀疏非厄米矩阵 $f(A)b$ 计算中，标准 Krylov 子空间方法因迭代步数增加导致存储和计算开销过大的问题，特别是 Arnoldi 过程需要存储全部基向量且受限于正交化成本。

Method: 提出了一个 sketch-and-restart 框架，将基于求积的重启策略与 sketched Arnoldi 过程及本文新提出的 sketched harmonic Arnoldi 过程相结合。第一类算法采用固定 Krylov 子空间维度；第二类算法通过截断 Arnoldi 过程自适应选择维度，利用 sketch 估计生成基的条件数，超过预设阈值时触发重启。

Result: 在正实矩阵的 Stieltjes 函数情形下，证明了 sketched harmonic Arnoldi 重启方法的收敛性。数值实验表明：sketching 带来了计算量节省，自适应截断减少了存储需求，厚重启（thick restarting）加速了收敛。

Conclusion: 该 sketch-and-restart 框架为大规模非厄米矩阵函数计算提供了高效可行的方案，兼具理论保证与实际性能优势。

Abstract: We develop a sketch-and-restart framework for computing the action of a matrix function on a vector, $f(A) b$, where $A$ is large, sparse, and non-Hermitian. The framework combines quadrature-based restarting with Arnoldi-like decompositions generated by sketched or truncated Arnoldi processes. Within this framework, we develop two classes of restarted algorithms. The first uses a fixed Krylov subspace dimension and is based either on the sketched Arnoldi process or on a new sketched harmonic Arnoldi process proposed in this work. The second class chooses the Krylov subspace dimension adaptively by running the truncated Arnoldi process until the condition number of the generated basis, estimated from its sketch, exceeds a prescribed threshold. We also establish the convergence of the restarted sketched harmonic Arnoldi method for Stieltjes functions under the assumption that $A$ is positive real. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, including the computational savings achieved through sketching, the storage reduction enabled by adaptive truncation, and the acceleration obtained from thick restarting.

</details>


### [259] [Convergence analysis of a nonlinear eigensolver based on rational approximation of the resolvent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10377)
*Nian Shao,Yuji Nakatsukasa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于草图化（sketched）预解式的有理逼近方法在线性和非线性特征值问题中的收敛性，证明了两种提高精度技术（块探测和缩放）的有效性，并建立了通过广义特征问题求解重心有理形式的极点前后向稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于草图化预解式的有理逼近方法能灵活求解线性和非线性特征值问题，但计算精度受限且收敛性缺乏理论分析。

Method: 理论分析收敛性，论证块探测（block probing）和缩放（zooming in）两种提高精度技术的有效性，并通过广义特征问题建立重心有理形式的极点前后向稳定性。

Result: 证明了块探测和缩放技术能有效提高计算精度，建立了重心有理形式极点求解的稳定性的理论保证，数值实验验证了理论结果的尖锐性。

Conclusion: 该方法可灵活求解线性和非线性特征值问题，且通过适当技术可有效提升精度，理论分析为实际应用提供了可靠依据。

Abstract: Given a holomorphic matrix-valued function, the poles of its sketched resolvent are generically its eigenvalues. Once a good rational approximation of the sketched resolvent is obtained, the poles of this rational approximation typically lie close to those eigenvalues, thus providing a flexible framework for solving both linear and nonlinear eigenvalue problems. However, the accuracy of the computed eigenvalues is limited and remains poorly understood. This paper analyzes the convergence of this approach and demonstrates the effectiveness of two techniques to improve accuracy: block probing and zooming in. We also establish the backward and forward stability of polefinding for a barycentric rational form via a generalized eigenproblem. Numerical experiments demonstrate the sharpness of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [260] [On the First Derivative Bounds for Rational Bézier Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10425)
*Mao Shi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了n≤6时有理Bézier曲线一阶导数的线性上界猜想成立，并通过实量词消去法精确验证，该边界可在线性时间内计算；同时指出n=7时该猜想不成立并给出最坏常数的计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有理Bézier曲线一阶导数线性上界猜想的最后一个低阶开放情形（n≤6），该猜想长期以来未被完全证明。

Method: 将问题重新表述为紧致箱体上的方差类函数最大化；采用分块论证证明最优解仅出现在一维面上，将问题简化为有限族多项式不等式；通过实量词消去法精确验证这些不等式。

Result: 证明了n≤6时线性界‖R'(t)‖ ≤ nΩmax‖Δ_i‖成立；指出n=7时该猜想不成立；边界可在O(n)时间内计算。

Conclusion: 该线性上界在低阶情形（n≤6）有效且计算高效，适合实时几何处理；高阶情形需要更精细的常数分析。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate sharp upper bounds for the first derivative of rational Bézier curves. A long-standing conjecture posited that the linear bound $\|\mathbf{R}'(t)\| \le nΩ\max\|Δ_i\|$ holds for all degrees. We prove that the bound is indeed valid for $n \leq 6$, thus resolving the last open low-degree case. The problem is reformulated as maximizing a variance-like function over a compact box. Using a block argument we show that optima can only appear on one-dimensional faces, reducing the task to a finite family of polynomial inequalities, which are verified exactly via real quantifier elimination. A notable practical feature is that the bound can be evaluated in linear time with respect to the degree, making it attractive for real-time geometric processing. The same structural analysis illustrates the failure for $n=7$ and outlines how the true worst-case constant can be computed.

</details>


### [261] [Computing critical velocities in waveguides via multiparameter eigenvalue problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10462)
*Hauke Gravenkamp,Xiang Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接计算波导动力学和移动载荷问题中临界速度的方法，无需追踪色散曲线，通过构建奇异多项式多参数特征值问题并线性化求解，可同时计算所有临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在波导动力学和移动载荷问题（如高速列车）中，临界速度标志着强振动放大的起始。在运动方向不变的系统中，这些速度可从色散关系中识别为传播模式的相速度和群速度相等的点。然而，通过追踪色散曲线间接寻找这些点对于具有复杂分支相互作用的多模态系统而言既繁琐又可能不可靠。

Method: 从波数-频率关系的多项式参数依赖特征值问题出发，引入相速度和群速度相等的附加条件，构建一个奇异多项式多参数特征值问题，然后将其线性化并使用已有算法求解。

Result: 通过多个基准问题的数值实验，验证了该方法能够准确、鲁棒地识别临界速度，且无需追踪色散曲线即可同时计算所有临界点。

Conclusion: 该方法为计算复杂多模态系统中的临界速度提供了一种直接、可靠且高效的途径，避免了传统色散曲线追踪方法带来的繁琐性和潜在不可靠性。

Abstract: In waveguide dynamics and moving-load problems (e.g., high-speed trains), critical velocities indicate the onset of strong vibration amplification. In systems that are invariant in the direction of motion, these velocities can be identified from dispersion relations as points where the phase and group velocities of a propagating mode coincide. Finding such points indirectly by tracing dispersion curves can be cumbersome and potentially unreliable for multimodal systems with complex branch interactions. We present a direct method for computing critical velocities in such scenarios, specifically in the context of semi-analytical methods. Starting from a polynomial parameter-dependent eigenvalue problem for the wavenumber-frequency relation, incorporating the additional condition of equal phase and group velocities yields a singular polynomial multiparameter eigenvalue problem that can be linearized and solved using established algorithms. The proposed approach enables the simultaneous computation of all critical points without requiring the tracing of dispersion curves. Its performance is demonstrated by several benchmark problems, confirming the accurate and robust identification of critical velocities.

</details>


### [262] [Comparing algebraic cubature rules on spline curved elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10485)
*A. Sommariva,M. Vianello*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种基于Green定理和多项式逼近理论（高斯求积、Tchakaloff定理、离散Chebyshev展开、Fekete型插值）的代数曲面积分公式构造方法，用于边界由样条跟踪的平面区域，并讨论了这些方法在弯曲多胞元方法中的优劣，同时提供了开源Matlab代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弯曲多胞元方法提供高效的代数曲面积分公式构造方案，并系统比较不同方法的适用性与局限性。

Method: 基于Green定理将面积分转化为边界线积分，结合四种多项式逼近技术：高斯求积、Tchakaloff定理、离散Chebyshev展开（超插值）、Fekete型插值，在样条跟踪边界的平面区域上构造代数曲面积分公式。

Result: 完成了四种方法的系统比较，讨论了各自在弯曲多胞元方法应用中的优势与不足，并公开了对应的Matlab代码库。

Conclusion: 四种方法各有适用场景，选择需权衡精度、稳定性和计算效率；开源代码便于领域研究者复现和进一步开发。

Abstract: We compare four methods for the construction of algebraic cubature rules on planar elements, whose boundary is tracked by splines. The methods, that we have developed over the last two decades, are based on Green theorem together with some cornerstones of polynomial approximation theory: Gaussian quadrature,Tchakaloff theorem, discretized Chebyshev expansion (hyperinterpolation), Fekete-like interpolation. We discuss their advantages and drawbacks in view of the application to curved polytopal element methods. We have also made freely available at a single site the corresponding open-source Matlab codes.

</details>


### [263] [Walk-on-Cubes Monte Carlo Simulation for nonisotropic fractional Laplace, Helmholtz, and Yukawa equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10528)
*Antti Rasila,Tommi Sottinen,Yaotong Yuan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了非各向同性分数阶拉普拉斯、亥姆霍兹和汤川方程，建立了Duffin对应与Feynman-Kac重构，并基于对称α-稳定Lévy过程提出了Walk-on-Cubes模拟算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非各向同性分数阶偏微分方程（拉普拉斯、亥姆霍兹、汤川方程），利用其与对称α-稳定Lévy过程的联系建立求解框架。

Method: 利用非各向同性分数阶拉普拉斯方程与对称α-稳定Lévy过程（独立同分布分量）的关系，建立汤川方程的Duffin对应和亥姆霍兹方程的Feynman-Kac重构，并提出Walk-on-Cubes算法进行数值模拟。

Result: 成功将分数阶亥姆霍兹和汤川方程与随机过程联系，并实现了Walk-on-Cubes算法的数值求解。

Conclusion: 非各向同性分数阶方程可通过Lévy过程的概率方法有效求解，Walk-on-Cubes算法适用于高阶分数阶方程。

Abstract: We study the nonisotropic fractional analogs of Laplace, Helmholtz and Yukawa equations. We provide a Duffin correspondence for the Yukawa equation and a Feynman--Kac reconstruction for the Helmholtz equation. The foundation of our analysis is the fact that the nonisotropic fractional Laplace equation is related to a symmetric $α$-stable Lévy process with independent identically distributed components. By using this relation we provide a Walk-on-Cubes algorithm that simulates the solutions of Helmholtz and Yukawa equations.

</details>


### [264] [JD-V: A new variant of Jacobi--Davidson method for large Hermitian eigenproblems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10635)
*Jinzhi Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型Jacobi-Davidson变体方法JD-V，通过重新设计修正方程，在保持子空间扩展效果的同时显著提高内迭代效率，尤其适用于高度聚集特征值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JD方法在求解高度聚集特征值问题时内迭代效率低下，需要设计一种更高效的修正方程以加速收敛。

Method: 设计新型修正方程，推导其与标准修正方程的等价性；利用MINRES方法进行严格收敛分析；开发带厚重启、紧缩和净化技术的JD-V算法以计算多个特征对。

Result: 理论分析和数值实验证实JD-V的内迭代效率显著优于标准JD，尤其在高度聚集特征值场景下整体效率大幅提升。

Conclusion: JD-V是一种高效的新型JD变体方法，在Hermitian特征值问题中具有显著优势。

Abstract: A novel variant of the Jacobi-Davidson (JD) type method for Hermitian eigenvalue problems, designated as JD-V, is proposed based on a newly designed correction equation, whose solution is shown to be nearly as effective as that of the standard correction equation for subspace expansion. Rigorous convergence analysis of MINRES for solving these equations reveals that the inner iterations of JD-V are significantly more efficient than those of the standard JD method when highly clustered eigenvalues are of interest. A thick-restart JD-V algorithm with deflation and purgation is developed to compute several eigenpairs of a a large-scale Hermitian matrix. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results and demonstrate the considerable superiority of JD-V over standard JD in overall efficiency.

</details>


### [265] [Symplectic Hamiltonian Direct Discontinuous Galerkin Method for Wave Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10652)
*Haomiao Li,Yumiao Li,Jiaxin Wang,Tiegang Liu,Kun Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种辛哈密顿直接间断伽辽金(DDG)方法用于逼近波动传播问题，在无需辅助变量的DG框架下证明了数值通量双线性形式的对称性与离散哈密顿结构的存在性等价，并基于对称DDG(SDDG)空间离散与辛时间积分器构造了全离散辛格式，给出了半线性波动方程的误差估计，数值实验验证了理论收敛率及该方法在长时间能量守恒和精度方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在间断伽辽金框架中寻找具有离散哈密顿结构的方法，以实现波动问题长时间模拟中的能量守恒和高精度。

Method: 通过分析数值通量双线性形式的对称性与离散哈密顿结构之间的等价关系，确定SDDG方法具有该结构；将其与辛时间积分器结合构造全离散辛格式；对半线性波动方程推导误差估计。

Result: SDDG方法具有离散哈密顿结构而Baumann-Oden、DDG、BR2等方法不具备；SDDG对位移具有最优收敛率，对速度具有次优收敛率；数值实验验证了理论结果并展示了辛哈密顿DDG方法在长时间能量守恒和精度上的优越性。

Conclusion: 辛哈密顿DDG方法是一种有效且可靠的长时间波动问题求解框架，能同时保证能量守恒特性和高精度。

Abstract: This paper presents a symplectic Hamiltonian direct discontinuous Galerkin (DDG) method for approximating wave propagation problems, including the linear and semilinear wave equations. Within an auxiliary-variable-free DG framework, we prove that the symmetry of the numerical flux bilinear form is equivalent to the existence of a discrete Hamiltonian structure. It follows that methods such as the symmetric interior penalty method and the symmetric DDG (SDDG) method admit a discrete Hamiltonian structure, whereas schemes including the Baumann--Oden, DDG, and BR2 methods do not possess this property. Exploiting this structure, we construct fully discrete symplectic schemes by combining the SDDG spatial discretization with symplectic time integrators. We further derive error estimates for the SDDG method applied to semilinear wave equations, showing the optimal convergence rate for the displacement and the suboptimal convergence rate for the velocity. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate that the symplectic Hamiltonian DDG method achieves superior long-time energy conservation and accuracy.

</details>


### [266] [A four-field auxiliary reformulation of a Cahn-Hilliard time step: Analysis and conforming finite element discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10724)
*Marvin Fritz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种四场辅助重构方法用于时间离散的Cahn-Hilliard步，通过标量场p和向量场u将标量量-Δc表示为2p+div u形式，形成混合二阶Stokes/弹性系统，并给出相容有限元离散、先验估计及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受二维Rafetseder-Zulehner分解中蕴含的标量迹结构启发，旨在构造一种新的辅助重构格式，以更好地处理Cahn-Hilliard方程时间离散步中的拉普拉斯项恢复问题。

Method: 提出四场辅助变分重构：引入辅助标量场p和向量场u，将-Δc表示为2p+div u；形成混合二阶Stokes/弹性型系统；保持相场演化的标准质量守恒梯度流结构；推导等价的连续四场格式；给出协调有限元离散并证明先验估计。

Result: 证明了相场变量的单步空间估计及包含显式弱拉普拉斯恢复缺陷的稳定辅助块估计；数值实验验证了相场收敛阶、质量守恒、能量耗散及辅助块的投影一致性。

Conclusion: 该四场辅助重构方法在理论上等价于经典凸分裂混合时间步格式，数值上实现了预期的相场收敛性和物理守恒特性，为Cahn-Hilliard方程的高效数值求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a four-field auxiliary reformulation of a time-discrete Cahn-Hilliard step. The construction is motivated by the scalar trace structure underlying two-dimensional Rafetseder-Zulehner decompositions and represents the scalar quantity $-Δc$ in the form $2p+div\,u$ through an auxiliary scalar field $p$ and an auxiliary vector field $u$. The resulting auxiliary problem is a mixed second-order Stokes/elasticity-type system, while the evolution equation for the phase field retains its standard mass-conserving gradient-flow structure. We derive a continuous four-field formulation that is equivalent to the classical convex-splitting mixed time step. We also state a conforming finite element discretization and prove one-step spatial estimates for the phase-field variables together with a stable auxiliary block estimate containing an explicit weak-Laplacian recovery defect. The numerical experiments verify the expected phase-field convergence in a manufactured setting, mass conservation, energy decay, and projected consistency of the auxiliary block.

</details>


### [267] [A compact simple HWENO scheme with ADER time discretization for hyperbolic conservation laws II: triangular meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10809)
*Dongmi Luo,Zhuang Zhao,Jianxian Qiu,Yibing Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在三角形网格上提出了一种结合ADER时间离散的紧致高精度HWENO格式，用于求解双曲守恒律。该方法通过Lax-Wendroff过程将时间导数转化为空间导数，并利用Green-Gauss定理从高斯点处的时精度解获得解的导数的单元平均值。与现有的RK-HWENO方法相比，新方法避免了求解额外演化方程、每个时间步只需一次重构，因此数值误差更小、计算成本更低、效率更高。与同阶精度的ADER-WENO方法相比，由于同时使用函数值及其一阶导数值进行重构，新方法的模板更紧致。数值算例验证了该方法在光滑解处的高精度和间断处的不振荡特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RK-HWENO方法在非结构化网格上需要求解额外的导数演化方程，且每个时间步需进行多次重构，导致计算效率较低。同时，现有ADER-WENO方法模板不够紧致。因此，本文旨在将结构化网格上的ADER-HWENO方法扩展到三角形网格，以克服上述局限。

Method: 采用ADER（Arbitrary High order using DERivatives）时间离散，利用Lax-Wendroff过程将时间导数转化为空间导数；通过Green-Gauss定理在单元界面高斯点处获得时精度解，进而得到导数的单元平均值，避免了直接演化导数方程；每个时间步仅进行一次HWENO重构；重构时同时使用函数值及其一阶导数值，以保持模板的紧致性。

Result: 数值实验表明，新方法在光滑区域时空精度均达到高阶，且在间断附近保持不振荡特性。与RK-HWENO方法相比，新方法数值误差更小、计算成本更低、效率更高。

Conclusion: 本文成功将ADER-HWENO方法从结构化网格推广至三角形网格，验证了其在非结构化网格上的有效性，为高效高精度求解双曲守恒律提供了新途径。

Abstract: A compact and high order HWENO scheme using ADER (Arbitrary high order using DERivatives) time discretization is developed for hyperbolic conservation laws on the triangular mesh, which is the extension of the work on the structured mesh (Luo et. al. (2024) \cite{luo2023}). The Lax-Wendroff procedure is employed to convert time derivatives to spatial derivatives. Thanks to this, the cell averages of the derivatives of the solution can be obtained by the time accurate solution as Gaussian points along the cell interfaces through the Green-Gauss theorem instead of by the evolution solution directly in the conventional HWENO methods. Comparing with the existing Runge-Kutta HWENO (RK-HWENO) method on the unstructured mesh (Zhao et. al. (2025) \cite{zhao2025}), the new method has the following advantages. Firstly, the RK-HWENO method must solve the additional equations for reconstructions and time advancing, which is avoided for the new method. Secondly, the HWENO reconstruction in the new method is performed once per time step and is different from the RK-HWENO method, in which the reconstruction is performed several times every time step. Because of these advantages the new method is more efficient than the RK-HWENO method with smaller numerical errors and less computational costs. Besides, comparing with the existing ADER-WENO methods \cite{dumbser20071,dumbser20072} under the same order of accuracy, the stencil of the new method is more compact since the both the function and its first derivative values are used in the reconstruction of the HWENO schemes. Numerical examples demonstrate that the new method can achieve the high order for smooth solutions both in space and time, keep non-oscillatory near discontinuities.

</details>


### [268] [Solving the Stokes Equations via a Least Squares Weak Galerkin Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10831)
*Chunmei Wang,Shangyou Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解 Stokes 方程的最小二乘弱伽辽金（LS-WG）有限元方法，该方法兼容任意多边形/多面体网格，通过最小二乘框架将传统不定鞍点问题转化为对称正定（SPD）线性系统，无需满足 inf-sup (LBB) 条件。理论推导了最优阶误差估计，数值实验验证了收敛率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Stokes 方程有限元求解面临两个核心困难：一是标准混合格式受限于 inf-sup (LBB) 相容性条件，导致速度与压力近似空间的选择受到严格约束；二是在任意多边形/多面体网格（如复杂几何区域、非结构化网格）上的离散面临挑战。现有弱伽辽金（WG）方法虽能处理复杂网格，但混合格式仍为不定鞍点系统，求解效率受限。因此，本文旨在设计一种无 LBB 限制、可在任意网格上生成 SPD 系统的 Stokes 数值格式。

Method: 提出最小二乘弱伽辽金（LS-WG）有限元方法。核心思路：(1) 在间断多项式空间上定义离散弱导数，使方法自然适用于任意多边形/多面体网格；(2) 采用最小二乘变分形式替代传统混合格式，将不定鞍点问题转化为对称正定（SPD）离散线性系统，从而绕过 inf-sup 条件；(3) 速度场采用 k 次多项式，压力采用 k-1 次多项式。在此基础上建立了格式的适定性，并在自定义离散能量范数下推导最优阶误差估计，理论证明了离散投影误差 𝒪(hᵏ) 和全局逼近误差 𝒪(hᵏ⁻¹) 的收敛率。

Result: 理论方面：严格证明了数值格式的适定性（well-posedness），并给出了最优阶误差估计——当速度场使用 k≥1 次多项式时，离散投影误差收敛率为 𝒪(hᵏ)，全局逼近误差收敛率为 𝒪(hᵏ⁻¹)。数值实验方面：大量算例验证了理论收敛率的正确性，同时展示了方法对复杂几何区域和一般网格剖分的鲁棒性、几何灵活性和求解效率。

Conclusion: LS-WG 方法成功克服了 Stokes 方程有限元求解中 LBB 条件的限制，并在任意多边形/多面体网格上实现了 SPD 系统的高效求解。理论分析与数值结果一致表明，该方法兼具几何灵活性、理论严谨性和计算效率，为不可压缩流动问题的数值模拟提供了一种稳健的新框架。

Abstract: We present a least-squares weak Galerkin (LS-WG) finite element method for solving the Stokes equations on arbitrary polygonal and polyhedral meshes. By utilizing discrete weak derivatives on discontinuous polynomial spaces, the proposed framework naturally accommodates complex domain geometries and general partitions. Crucially, this least-squares formulation bypasses the traditional inf-sup (LBB) compatibility condition, transforming the standard indefinite saddle-point problem into an inherently symmetric and positive definite (SPD) discrete linear system. We establish the well-posedness of the numerical scheme and rigorously derive optimal-order error estimates in a custom discrete energy norm. Specifically, we prove convergence rates of $\mathcal{O}(h^k)$ for the discrete projection error and $\mathcal{O}(h^{k-1})$ for the global approximation error when employing polynomials of degree $k \ge 1$ for the velocity field and $k-1$ for the pressure. Extensive numerical experiments confirm these theoretical convergence rates, demonstrating the method's robustness, geometric flexibility, and overall efficiency.

</details>


### [269] [Structure-Preserving Neural ODEs via Nonstandard Finite Difference Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10858)
*Achraf Zinihi,Matthias Ehrhardt,Moulay Rchid Sidi Ammi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非标准有限差分离散的结构保持神经ODE框架，通过非负生产率和销毁率参数化动力学，无条件保持正性，并基于Patankar型离散化扩展以精确保持守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经ODE在学习连续时间动力学时无法保持正性等重要定性性质，限制了其在需要物理一致性的应用场景中的使用。

Method: 采用非标准有限差分离散化方法，将学习的动力学参数化为非负的生产率和销毁率，构造显式可微更新；并通过Patankar型离散化扩展实现守恒律的精确保持。

Result: 在SIR流行病模型上的数值实验表明，所提方法能生成物理有意义的轨迹，在粗离散化下保持鲁棒性，在保持学习动力学的定性结构方面优于传统神经ODE。

Conclusion: 该结构保持的神经ODE框架能够无条件保持正性和守恒律，为需要定性保真度的连续时间动力学学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Although neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs) are a powerful framework for learning continuous-time dynamics, they generally do not preserve essential qualitative properties, such as positivity. We propose a structure-preserving Neural ODE framework based on nonstandard finite difference (NSFD) discretization. The learned dynamics are parameterized by nonnegative production and destruction rates, yielding an explicit, differentiable update that integrates seamlessly into standard automatic differentiation pipelines. We prove that the resulting scheme unconditionally preserves positivity for arbitrary time-step sizes while retaining first-order consistency. We outline an extension based on Patankar-type discretizations that preserves conservation laws exactly. Numerical experiments on an SIR epidemic model show that our approach generates physically meaningful trajectories, remains robust under coarse discretizations, and outperforms conventional NODEs in preserving the qualitative structure of the learned dynamics.

</details>


### [270] [Multilevel Preconditioning Strategies for Convex Optimization Methods in Image Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10864)
*Stefano Aleotti,Claudia Binda,Marco Donatelli,Rolf Krause*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合预处理（preconditioning）与多层（multilevel）策略的加速框架，用于标准和非精确前向-后向算法求解正则化凸优化问题，在图像去模糊实验中显著提升了收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端梯度方法在成像领域广泛应用，现有研究分别证明了预处理策略和多层框架各自能加速收敛，但尚未将两者结合以发挥协同加速效果。

Method: 将预处理（variable metrics/preconditioning）与多层框架（multilevel）相结合，应用于标准前向-后向算法和非精确前向-后向算法，构建一个鲁棒且一致的加速框架。

Result: 在图像去模糊问题上的数值实验表明，所提出方法相比标准方法在收敛速度上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 预处理与多层策略的结合能够有效加速前向-后向算法的收敛，在正则化凸优化问题（如图像去模糊）中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Proximal gradient methods are widely used in imaging, and their speed of convergence can be accelerated by incorporating variable metrics and/or extrapolation steps. Recent works have shown that preconditioning strategies can significantly enhance this acceleration, in particular, for image deblurring problems. In parallel, a multilevel framework has been introduced to speed up inertial and inexact forward-backward schemes for image restoration problems. In this paper, we combine preconditioning and multilevel strategies to design a robust and consistent acceleration framework for both standard and inexact forward-backward schemes applied to regularized convex optimization problems. Numerical experiments in image deblurring confirm that our approach yields a substantial improvement in convergence speed compared to standard methods.

</details>


### [271] [Performance Evaluation of Stabilized Corrections for Mixed Precision Runge--Kutta Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10967)
*César Herrera,John Driscoll,Sigal Gottlieb,Zachary J. Grant,Tej Sai Kakumanu,Andrew Christlieb*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合精度Runge-Kutta方法中稳定化校正的运行时性能，通过非线性PDE数值实验证明稳定化校正能在保持显著加速的同时提高混合精度格式的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度Runge-Kutta方法通过低精度计算降低DIRK格式中隐式求解的成本，但校正步骤会损害稳定性。此前稳定化校正的运行时成本尚未被量化，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 使用无粘Burgers方程和孔隙介质方程的谱半离散化，对比未校正、显式校正和稳定化校正的混合精度DIRK方法，涵盖半精度、单精度、双精度和四精度组合，针对二阶至四阶SDIRK格式进行数值实验。

Result: 稳定化校正提高了混合精度格式的精度，同时保持了显著的运行时节省。所有实验在Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+ CPU上使用Julia 1.11.4完成。

Conclusion: 稳定化校正是有效的混合精度策略，能够在不牺牲运行时效率的前提下恢复精度损失。

Abstract: Mixed precision Runge--Kutta methods reduce the cost of the expensive implicit solves in diagonally implicit Runge--Kutta (DIRK) schemes by evaluating them in low precision, while retaining the accuracy of the scheme for larger time steps. The accuracy lost to the low-precision perturbation can be recovered through inexpensive explicit corrections; however, these corrections have an adverse impact on stability. Recently proposed stabilized corrections remedy this by applying a stabilization matrix to the correction step, but their runtime cost has not previously been quantified. In this work, we present a numerical study of the runtime performance of these stabilized corrections. Using spectral semi-discretizations of two nonlinear partial differential equations, the inviscid Burgers' equation and the porous medium equation, we compare uncorrected mixed precision DIRK methods against explicitly corrected and stabilized variants across half, single, double, and quadruple precision pairings, for SDIRK methods of orders two through four. We report convergence, runtime, and speedups, and show that the stabilized corrections improve the accuracy of the mixed precision schemes while preserving substantial runtime savings. All experiments were performed on an Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+ CPU with Julia version 1.11.4.

</details>


### [272] [An Edge-Based Formulation for the Exact Computation of High-Order Zernike Moments of 2D Shapes and Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11158)
*Patrice Koehl,Stephan Tillmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于边缘的Zernike矩计算方法，通过格林定理将区域积分转化为边界线积分，消除了传统像素基方法的高阶空间混叠误差，适用于多边形、二值、灰度及彩色图像，并利用Clenshaw-Curtis求积实现精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Zernike矩基于像素中心点质量的数值求积存在空间混叠误差，随着阶数增加误差加剧，导致图像重建质量下降和高阶矩判别能力减弱。需要一种能从根本上消除该误差的计算方法。

Method: 1) 应用格林定理将Zernike矩的二维面积积分转化为沿图像边界的线积分；2) 推导径向基元的递推关系；3) 证明变换后的边界被积函数为多项式，采用Clenshaw-Curtis求积实现精确数值计算。

Result: 在图像重建、形状分析和字符分类实验中，所提方法在低阶矩时与经典方法精度相当，而在高阶矩时保持稳定，传统像素基方法则遭受显著的混叠和数值退化。

Conclusion: 基于边缘的Zernike矩计算消除了空间离散化误差，在高阶矩计算中保持数值稳定性和精度，是一种实用且精确的替代方案。

Abstract: Zernike moments are widely used rotation-invariant descriptors for shape and image analysis, but their standard computation relies on a pixel-based quadrature that treats each pixel as a point mass located at its center. This approximation introduces spatial aliasing that increases with moment order, degrading image reconstruction and reducing the discriminative power of high-order moments. We present an edge-based formulation that eliminates this source of error by applying Green's theorem to transform the two-dimensional area integral defining a Zernike moment into a sum of one-dimensional integrals along image boundaries. The resulting framework applies equally to polygonal shapes, binary images, grayscale images, and color images. We derive recurrence relations for the required radial primitives and show that the transformed edge integrands are polynomial functions, allowing their exact evaluation using Clenshaw--Curtis quadrature. The proposed method computes Zernike moments from polygonal image representations without the spatial discretization errors inherent to conventional pixel-based approaches and remains computationally practical for high-order moments. Numerical experiments on image reconstruction, shape analysis, and character classification demonstrate that the proposed formulation matches the accuracy of classical methods at low orders while remaining stable at orders for which pixel-based moments suffer from significant aliasing and numerical degradation.

</details>


### [273] [Adaptive Krylov Methods for Low-Rank Exponential Integrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11293)
*Rico Weigel,Tom-Christian Riemer,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用框架，将求解刚性时变常微分方程的指数积分器（KIOPS和RK2EXPINT）扩展到张量列（TT）格式，通过增广刚度张量和Krylov子空间方法，在低秩场景下实现了相比经典矩阵方法显著的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典指数积分器（如KIOPS和RK2EXPINT）假设系统由矩阵和向量表示，未能利用潜在的高维结构；张量及其TT分解具有显著的存储效率优势，但缺乏适用于刚性ODE求解的通用框架。

Method: 1) 将显式指数龙格-库塔积分器的方案重新表述为张量形式；2) 增广刚度张量，通过单次指数函数评估（利用Krylov子空间方法）计算作用于张量的φ-函数线性组合；3) 将原始矩阵方法的理论（关键定理）推广到张量情形。

Result: 数值实验表明，在低秩场景下，KIOPS-TT和RK2EXPINT-TT相比经典矩阵方法取得了显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的TT格式框架有效解决了经典指数积分器无法利用高维结构的问题，在保持理论有效性的同时大幅提升了低秩场景下的计算效率。

Abstract: Differential equations arise in numerous applications, particularly within scientific and technical contexts. Systems of stiff, time-dependent ordinary differential equations constitute the focus of this work. Exponential integrators are designed to solve such equations by integrating the linear part exactly, while simultaneously approximating the nonlinear part through a linear combination of $\varphi$-functions. By utilizing an augmented stiffness matrix, state-of-the-art methods like KIOPS and RK2EXPINT solve the linear part and evaluate linear combinations of $\varphi$-functions for the nonlinear part in a single step, effectively reducing the computational effort to a single matrix exponential evaluation. However, these classical approaches assume that the system is represented by matrices and vectors, potentially not utilizing the underlying high-dimensional structure. Tensors address this limitation and offer significant storage efficiency through well-established decompositions like the Tensor Train (TT) format. This work provides a general framework for solving stiff, time-dependent systems directly within the TT format. Specifically, KIOPS-TT and RK2EXPINT-TT are developed as extensions of the original KIOPS and RK2EXPINT algorithms. This involves reformulating the scheme of explicit exponential Runge-Kutta integrators for tensors and augmenting the stiffness tensor to compute linear combinations of $\varphi$-functions acting on tensors through a single evaluation of the exponential function using Krylov subspace methods. Furthermore, it is shown that the underlying theory of the matrix methods remains valid, thereby enabling the transfer of key theorems to the tensor case. Numerical experiments confirm significant speed-ups for KIOPS-TT and RK2EXPINT-TT in low-rank scenarios compared to their classical counterparts.

</details>


### [274] [Analysis-aware interface coarsening for elliptic problems driven by an equilibrated flux estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11297)
*Denise Grappein,Philipp Weder*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对椭圆界面问题提出了一种基于平衡通量重构的后验误差估计器，用于分析感知的界面粗化决策。该估计器同时考虑了界面粗化带来的建模误差和离散近似产生的数值误差，并能在网格单元上局部化，显式追踪扩散系数对比度，从而指导哪些特征应被粗化、哪些应被保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将缺陷去除（defeaturing）概念从边界特征扩展到内部界面特征，旨在简化内部界面以降低网格划分难度和自由度数量，同时提供可量化的误差控制。

Method: 提出基于平衡通量重构的后验误差估计器，将误差分解为建模误差（界面粗化导致）和数值误差（离散近似导致），在网格单元上局部化，并显式追踪扩散系数对比度。通过平衡通量重构紧束缚数值误差源。

Result: 证明了估计器的可靠性，并通过多个数值算例验证，包括完全移除内部界面的极端情况。

Conclusion: 该估计器能够有效指导界面粗化决策，在给定网格尺寸下区分应粗化或保留的特征，实现建模误差与数值误差的联合控制。

Abstract: We propose an a posteriori error estimator that relies on an equilibrated flux reconstruction to enable analysis-aware interface coarsening decisions in elliptic interface problems. Interface coarsening consists of simplifying an internal interface that separates regions characterized by different physical properties in order to facilitate the meshing process and reduce the total number of degrees of freedom. This process extends the concept of defeaturing, where small features on the boundary of a computational domain are removed. Here, the features lie on an interior interface instead. The focus is on a Laplace problem with a discontinuous diffusion coefficient. The estimator accounts for both the modeling error arising from the interface coarsening and the numerical error from the discrete approximation of the solution to the coarsened-interface problem. It is localized on the mesh elements, and its constants explicitly track the contrast between the diffusion coefficients. The impact of different features can be assessed individually, yielding local contributions that indicate which features to coarsen and which to retain for a given mesh size. The use of an equilibrated flux reconstruction allows us to sharply bound the bulk numerical source of error. We prove the reliability of the estimator and verify it across several numerical examples, including the case where an internal interface is fully removed.

</details>


### [275] [Performance evaluation of branch-free fused multiply-add algorithms for multi-component-type multiple-precision floating-point arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11391)
*Tomonori Kouya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于无分支错误变换（EFT）的多精度浮点算术中的新型无分支融合乘加运算（FMA），覆盖双字（DW）、三字（TW）和四字（QW）精度，并通过基准测试证明其相比现有无分支算法能进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无分支算法（Zhang & Aiken）在DW/TW/QW加法与乘法上取得了性能提升，但缺乏相应的无分支融合乘加操作（FMA），导致在需要FMA的场景下性能受限，因此需要设计无分支FMA以进一步加速。

Method: 基于错误变换（EFT）技术，为DW、TW和QW算术设计无分支的融合乘加运算（FMA），消除条件分支以实现常数时间执行；并在实际基准测试中与现有无分支算法进行性能对比。

Result: 提出的无分支FMA操作在DW、TW、QW精度下均实现了性能提升，基准测试验证了其加速效果，尤其对TW和QW变体效果显著。

Conclusion: 无分支FMA能够有效弥补现有无分支多精度算术的操作缺口，进一步释放多分量多精度算术的性能潜力，适用于高性能计算中对延迟敏感的场景。

Abstract: High performance in multi-component multiple-precision arithmetic constructed from existing floating-point operations using error-free transformations (EFTs), can be achieved by employing branch-free algorithms that eliminate if-branches. Zhang and Aiken proposed branch-free algorithms for addition and multiplication in double-word(DW), triple-word(TW), and quadruple-word(QW) arithmetic. Among these, we implemented the TW and QW variants, for which substantial performance improvements over existing algorithms can be expected, and demonstrated that they indeed contribute to acceleration. In this paper, we propose new branch-free fused multiply-add operations for DW, TW, and QW arithmetic to achieve further performance improvements, and show through benchmark tests that the proposed operations can enhance performance.

</details>


### [276] [Convolution quadrature based on a truncated trapezoidal rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11422)
*Matteo Ferrari*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了截断梯形规则（一种介于BDF2和梯形规则之间的A-稳定二阶多步法族），推导了在A-稳定性约束下最小化主误差常数的闭式系数表达式，并证明了该常数随参数J增大而趋近于最优Dahlquist值1/12，且始终小于BDF2的常数。将该方法应用于卷积求积框架，其在单位闭圆邻域内的解析性使得正则性和扰动要求比梯形规则更宽松，而误差常数可通过增大J任意接近最优值。数值实验表明，基于截断梯形规则的卷积求积在符号扰动下保持稳定（梯形规则失效），同时误差常数小于BDF2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，梯形规则具有良好的稳定性但误差常数较大，且对正则性和扰动要求严格；BDF2虽稳定但误差常数不够小。需要一种在A-稳定性、误差常数和鲁棒性之间取得更好折中的数值方法。

Method: 研究了由整数J≥2参数化的截断梯形规则族（二阶多步法），推导了在A-稳定性约束下最小化主误差常数的闭式系数公式及对应的主误差常数显式表达式，并将其应用于卷积求积框架。

Result: 得到了最小化主误差常数的闭式系数表达式和显式误差常数公式；该常数随J增大单调递减至最优Dahlquist值1/12，且对所有J≥2均严格小于BDF2常数。在卷积求积中，该方法具有比梯形规则更宽松的正则性和扰动要求，数值实验表明其在符号扰动下保持稳定而梯形规则失败，且误差常数小于BDF2。

Conclusion: 截断梯形规则成功地在A-稳定性、误差常数和鲁棒性之间取得了优于BDF2和梯形规则的平衡，通过增大J可使误差常数任意接近理论最优值，是一种有效的卷积求积基方法。

Abstract: We study the truncated trapezoidal rule, a family of A-stable second order multistep methods parametrized by an integer $J \ge 2$, a compromise between BDF2 and the trapezoidal rule. We obtain a closed-form expression for the coefficients that minimize the principal error constant under the A-stability constraint and we derive an explicit formula for the corresponding principal error constant. The latter decreases to the optimal Dahlquist value $1/12$ as $J$ increases, and is strictly smaller than the BDF2 constant for every $J \ge 2$. We apply the truncated trapezoidal rule within the convolution quadrature framework. Its analyticity in a neighbourhood of the closed unit disk yields milder regularity and perturbation requirements than those of the trapezoidal rule, while the error constant can be made arbitrarily close to the optimal one by increasing $J$. Numerical experiments show that convolution quadrature based on the truncated trapezoidal rule remains stable under symbol perturbations, where the trapezoidal rule fails, while achieving a smaller error constant than BDF2.

</details>


### [277] [Algebraic Invariant Quadratization Schemes for Cahn--Hilliard Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11569)
*Fei Xie,Nan Lu,Yajuan Sun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了代数不变量二次化（AIQ）框架，通过引入辅助变量（解释为扩展系统的Casimir函数）处理类有理能量函数。将AIQ与辛Runge-Kutta（SRK）时间格式和Fourier伪谱空间离散结合，得到全离散格式，应用于各向同性和各向异性Cahn-Hilliard方程。分析了离散色散关系、旋节线失稳、粗化行为和缺失取向现象。数值比较表明，该方法在保持原始能量演化和捕捉物理现象方面优于S-IEQ和SAV方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IEQ和SAV方法在处理有理型能量函数时存在局限性，需要一种能更好保持原始能量演化特性的新框架。

Method: 提出AIQ框架，引入作为Casimir函数的辅助变量；结合SRK时间格式和Fourier伪谱空间离散；应用于各向同性和各向异性Cahn-Hilliard方程。

Result: 分析了离散色散关系、旋节线失稳、粗化行为和缺失取向现象；数值比较显示AIQ方法在能量演化保持和物理现象捕捉上优于S-IEQ和SAV方法。

Conclusion: AIQ框架结合SRK和Fourier伪谱离散是处理类有理能量函数Cahn-Hilliard方程的有效数值方案，性能优于现有IEQ类方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Algebraic Invariant Quadratization (AIQ) framework for rational-like energy functions by introducing auxiliary variables, which are interpreted as Casimir functions of the extended system. Combining AIQ with symplectic Runge--Kutta (SRK) methods in time and Fourier pseudo-spectral discretization in space, we obtain fully discrete schemes. The resulting schemes are applied to Cahn--Hilliard equations in both the isotropic and anisotropic cases. We analyze the discrete dispersion relation, spinodal instability, coarsening behavior, and missing-orientation phenomena. Numerical comparisons demonstrate the improved performance superiority of the proposed method over the stabilized invariant energy quadratization (S-IEQ) and scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) methods in preserving the original energy evolution and capturing the underlying physical phenomena.

</details>


### [278] [A Twin gradient method for unconstrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11575)
*Anna De Magistris,Michiel E. Hochstenbach,Gerardo Toraldo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双平行序列协同确定步长的无约束优化策略（Twin-Step），通过最小化两梯度过程间的欧氏距离选择步长。理论分析表明收敛性依赖于搜索方向夹角，为应对方向平行问题，引入混合框架Twin-ABBₘᵢₙ，在几何协作失效时切换至自适应Barzilai-Borwein方法。数值实验显示Twin阶段为后续BB型迭代创造了有利初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度优化方法中步长选择策略存在局限性，探索通过双序列协同机制自动确定步长的新途径，以提升优化效率。

Method: 提出Twin-Step策略：维护两条并行迭代序列，每步通过最小化两梯度过程间的欧氏距离来联合确定步长；为应对搜索方向接近平行时的失效问题，设计混合框架Twin-ABBₘᵢₙ，在必要时切换至Adaptive Barzilai-Borwein方法。

Result: 理论证明了互距离收敛性与搜索方向夹角的关系；大量数值实验表明Twin协作阶段能够为后续BB型迭代提供有利的初始条件。

Conclusion: Twin-Step策略通过双序列协同可有效指导步长选择，混合框架保证了算法的鲁棒性，具有较好的优化性能。

Abstract: We propose a new strategy for gradient-based unconstrained optimization, involving two parallel sequences of iterates that cooperate to determine their stepsizes via a \textit{Twin-Step} principle. Rather than minimizing the objective function individually, the algorithm selects steplengths that minimize the Euclidean distance between the two gradient based processes occurring simultaneously at each iteration. The theoretical analysis shows that the convergence of the mutual distance is governed by the angle between the search directions. In particular the effectiveness of the overall process degrades as the directions approach parallelism. To ensure robustness against collinearity, we introduce a hybrid framework, Twin-ABB$_{\min}$, which switches to the Adaptive Barzilai--Borwein method when the geometric cooperation becomes ineffective. Extensive and very promising numerical results evidence that the Twin phase creates favorable initial conditions for subsequent BB-type iterations.

</details>


### [279] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for the High-Resolution Reconstruction of Flow Measurement Indicators in Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11576)
*Irena Radišić,Raffaele Tirotta,Alberto Zingaro,Stefano Pagani,Luca Dede'*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物理信息的神经网络（PINN）框架，通过融入不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程约束，对稀疏、噪声的流速测量数据进行重建，提升速度场、压力及壁面剪切应力的空间分辨率，并在FDA喷嘴基准和动脉瘤模型上验证了其优于传统CFD和纯数据驱动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验血流测量技术（如4D flow MRI、PIV、多普勒超声）在血管壁附近及复杂流动区域常产生稀疏、含噪或分辨率不足的数据，限制了血流动力学指标（如壁面剪切应力）的准确评估和临床效用，亟需一种能融合物理规律与测量数据的方法来提升重建 fidelity。

Method: 采用物理信息神经网络（PINN），将不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程作为物理损失项嵌入网络训练，同时以实验速度测量数据作为数据损失项，通过联合优化实现速度场重建、压力及壁面剪切应力等未测量量的估计，并提升血流动力学指标的空间分辨率。

Result: 在FDA喷嘴基准上（使用控制PIV测量和CFD模拟）与动脉瘤模型（使用体外4D flow MRI数据）两个案例中，PINN方法相比标准CFD和纯数据驱动方法，重建结果与真实观测更吻合，证明了数据驱动学习与物理正则化协同的有效性。

Conclusion: PINN框架能够显著提升欠分辨率流速测量的 fidelity，提供空间解析的血流动力学指标，在心血管研究和临床实践中具有改善血流测量质量的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate, spatially resolved flow field measurements are essential for the reliable assessment of hemodynamic quantities in cardiovascular research and clinical practice. Experimental techniques, such as 4D flow MRI, PIV, or Doppler ultrasound, often yield data that are sparse, noisy, or under-resolved, particularly near vessel walls and in regions of complex flow. This limits the fidelity of distributed or derived hemodynamic indicators such as the wall shear stress and the clinical utility of such measurements. To address these challenges, we propose a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework that integrates the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations with velocity measurements coming from experimental flow field data. By embedding physical laws into data, PINN enhances the reconstruction of velocity fields, enables the estimation of unmeasured quantities such as pressure and wall shear stress, and improves the spatial resolution of hemodynamic indicators. We show the effectiveness of our approach using both in silico and experimental data. First, we apply our method to the FDA nozzle benchmark, leveraging both control particle image velocimetry (PIV) measurements and computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations. Next, we apply our method to the more complex case of blood flow in an aneurysm model, exploiting in vitro 4D flow MRI data. In both cases, the synergy between data-driven learning and physics-based regularization yields results that align more closely with ground truth observations than standard CFD or pure data-driven approaches. Our findings highlight the potential of PINNs to improve the fidelity of under-resolved flow field measurements and yield spatially resolved hemodynamic indicators.

</details>


### [280] [Inf-Sup Neural Networks for High Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11718)
*Ziren Chen,Hailiang Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经网络的框架，通过拉格朗日乘子将高维PDE重新表述为inf-sup优化问题，并采用双网络参数化和迭代鞍点优化求解，理论证明了与原问题的等价性并给出了误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维偏微分方程求解面临维度灾难的挑战，现有方法在维度升高时计算复杂度急剧增长，亟需能够有效处理高维问题的新方法。

Method: 将PDE重新表述为inf-sup优化问题，引入拉格朗日乘子；使用两个神经网络分别参数化原始解和拉格朗日乘子；通过迭代鞍点优化过程求解；从理论上证明了优化问题与原PDE的等价性，并推导了包含网络近似误差、统计（采样）误差和优化误差的完整误差估计。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在求解高维PDE时具有准确性、稳定性和高效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维PDE求解提供了一种理论上完备、实践上可行的神经网络方法，有效缓解了维度灾难问题。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations (PDEs) in high dimensions remains challenging due to the curse of dimensionality. We propose a neural-network-based framework that reformulates PDEs as inf--sup optimization problems through the introduction of a Lagrange multiplier. The primal solution and the associated Lagrange multiplier are parameterized by two networks and are computed via an iterative saddle-point optimization procedure. We prove the theoretical equivalence between the proposed optimization formulation and the original PDE problem, and we derive rigorous error estimates that quantify the total approximation error in terms of the network approximation error, statistical (sampling) error, and optimization error. Numerical experiments demonstrate the accuracy, stability, and efficiency of the proposed method for solving high-dimensional PDEs.

</details>
